Sources actually opened: EMBOSS 6.6 `ajalign.c`, `ajseqwrite.c`, `ajseqtype.c`, `ajseq.c`, `ajstr.c`, `embaln.c` (raw.githubusercontent.com); the installed needle / water / stretcher / seqret 6.6.0.
- **Fix (additive).** `EmbossAlignmentWriter` + `EmbossAlignFormat` / `EmbossAlignOptions` / `EmbossPairAlignment` / `EmbossAlignedSequence` (`EmbossAlignmentTypes.cs`); the similarity marks reuse the `MatrixClassifier` of `CalculateStatistics` (`SequenceAligner.EmbossColumnMarks`, internal), the exact `%.Nf` goes through `CFormat.Fixed`; MCP `write_alignment`. Quirks reproduced: `%2d` day in `Rundate`, `markx` running number widths, MEGA block clamping (`ajStrAssignSubS`), NEXUS `datatype=DNA` for untyped sets, stretcher integer scores / no descriptions / T-Coffee percent identity. `ClustalWAlignmentWriter` (ClustalW's own byte layouts) is a different dialect and is untouched; the shared pieces (GCG checksum idea, `CFormat`) are not duplicated further.
- **Reference cross-check.** `emboss_aformat_oracle.tsv` (3 303 files: needle / water / stretcher x 29 formats x 114 sets, faked dates via `scripts/alignment/faketime.c`) and `emboss_seqout_oracle.tsv` (1 309 `seqret` files, 11 formats, 1-9 rows): all identical. Rows the tool corrupts (uninitialised memory in the SAM CIGAR of a water `-aglobal` run, X to N conversion on reading) are excluded by the generators.
- **Not done (DOABLE, registered).** A2-27b: Biopython `Bio.Align.write` writers / readers (fasta, clustal, stockholm, phylip, a2m, nexus, maf, psl, sam, bed, exonerate, mauve, chain) and readers for the formats above, MCP `format` parameter on the other tools, T-Coffee `.score_ascii`; pair-markup formats for 3+ rows (need EMBOSS `cons`).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_EmbossFormats_Tests` (6), MCP `EmbossFormatTests` (5). Commit a9e3d2c0.

### F121 — ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 (audit A2-28a, WP32): EMBOSS stretcher ported; 689 / 689 pairs equal
- **Finding.** The F46 linear-space program has different tie rules; no stretcher parity. Source: `emboss/stretcher.c` (opened), installed stretcher 6.6.0.
- **Fix (additive).** `SequenceAligner.StretcherAlign(seq1, seq2, matrix, gapOpen, gapExtend)` (`SequenceAligner.Stretcher.cs`): Myers-Miller with the program's integer arithmetic, midpoint rules, `STRETCHERDEL/INS/REP` script and `stretcher_Calcons`; MCP `stretcher_align`.
- **Cross-check.** `stretcher_oracle.tsv` 689 / 689 (rows + score); stretcher full files are part of F120's 3 303.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_Stretcher_Tests` (5). Commit a9e3d2c0.

### F122 — ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 / ALIGN-LOCAL-001 (audit A2-28b, WP32): needle option set (-endweight / -endopen / -endextend) and water ported in the programs' single precision
- **Finding.** Needle's end-gap options and the exact tie order of needle / water rows were not implemented (A1-3 remainder). Sources: `embaln.c` (`embAlignPathCalcWithEndGapPenalties`, `embAlignWalkNWMatrixUsingCompass`, `embAlignPathCalcSW`, `embAlignWalkSWMatrix`), `ajmath.c` `ajRoundFloat`, `ajdefine.h`.
- **Fix (additive).** `SequenceAligner.NeedleAlign(..., endWeight, endOpen, endExtend)` and `WaterAlign` with `RoundFloat` (16 mantissa bits: `-gapopen 0.1` is 0.10000038); floats and doubles exactly as in C.
- **Cross-check.** `needle_oracle.tsv` 887 / 887 (rows + printed score; 100+ with `-endweight`, fractional / non-dyadic penalties), `water_oracle.tsv` 860 / 860 (rows, starts, scores). Facts: the programs are not always optimal (forbidden gap-state transitions; one-residue needle boundary mis-initialisation, score 0): the affine optima are upper bounds reached on > 90 %.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_Needle_Tests` (6), `SequenceAligner_Water_Tests` (3). Commit a9e3d2c0.

### F123 — ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 / ALIGN-LOCAL-001 (audit A2-29, WP32): ksw2-style two-piece (dual) affine gap alignment, global / local, banded
- **Finding.** Only single-affine and the general-gap O(m n (m+n)) programs existed; no O(m n) two-piece model (ksw2 `extd2`, minimap2 `-O 4,24 -E 2,1`), no band. Sources: minimap2 / ksw2 description (WebSearch snippets), Gotoh 1982; the ksw2 C source was not compiled (no network binary; `mappy` not installed here).
- **Fix (additive).** `DualAffineScoring`, `SequenceAligner.GlobalAlignDualAffine` / `LocalAlignDualAffine(seq1, seq2, scoring, band)` (`SequenceAligner.DualAffine.cs`): Gotoh with H, E1, F1, E2, F2, banded by |i - j|; MCP `dual_affine_align`.
- **Cross-check.** `dual_affine_oracle.tsv` (scripts/alignment/generate_dual_affine_oracle.py): 1 600 cases (400 each global / local x full / banded); full-matrix scores = Biopython 1.88 PairwiseAligner with the callable gap score = an independent Waterman-Smith-Beyer Python program (asserted by the generator), which also gives the banded scores; every returned alignment re-scores to its score; 1 600 / 1 600 equal.
- **Not done (DOABLE, registered A2-29b).** Z-drop, end bonus and extension alignment (`ksw2_extz` / `extd2` extension mode), ambiguous-base (N) scoring, CIGAR parity with the real ksw2 / mappy (needs the C program: Docker package when taken up).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_DualAffine_Tests` (5), MCP `EmbossFormatTests.DualAffineAlign_...`. Commit 790e9170.

### F124 — ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 (audit A2-28c, WP33): EMBOSS supermatcher and needleall ported; 520 / 520 runs equal
- **Finding.** No `supermatcher` (Rabin-Karp word seeds + banded Smith-Waterman) and no `needleall`; the `-nobrief` sub-header of needle (`embAlignCalcSimilarity`) was documented in the writer but never computed. Sources actually opened (raw.githubusercontent.com, `Seqeron-review/1.0`): EMBOSS `supermatcher.c`, `needleall.c`, `needle.c`, `embword.c` (`embWordGetTable`, `embWordRabinKarpInit`, `embWordRabinKarpSearch`, `embWordMatchFirstMax`), `embaln.c` (`embAlignPathCalcSWFast`, `embAlignWalkSWMatrixFast`, `embAlignReportLocal`, `embAlignCalcSimilarity`), `ajalign.c` (format table, `ajAlignFormatShowsSequences`), `ajmem.h`, `ajdefine.h`; the installed 6.6.0 programs.
- **Fix (additive).** `SequenceAligner.Supermatcher(queries, targets, matrix, gapOpen, gapExtend, wordLength, width, minScore, outputFormat)` (`SequenceAligner.Supermatcher.cs`): the word table including the program's window skipping of `N` / `X` (a window is only tested at its last residue after a jump), the Rabin-Karp search with the `lastlocation` cursor and the match extension, the first longest match as seed, `supermatcher_findendpoints`, the single-precision banded path matrix and walk-back, the error-file line; `match` / `score` formats get the plain-rows alignment with the target first (`ajAlignDefineCC`). `SequenceAligner.NeedleAll` (+ `SimilaritySubHeader`, the `-nobrief` lines incl. the x86 `-nan`); `EmbossAlignmentWriter` now prints the program sub-header in the `match` / `score` layouts. MCP `supermatcher_align`, `needleall_align`. **Duplication removed:** the matrix / residue-code table of needle and water (and now supermatcher, needleall) is one class `EmbossScoreTable` (stretcher keeps its row-wise table: it needs per-residue score rows).
- **Facts established.** needleall's `-minscore` default is 0 (the ACD help text says 1.0); the programs read a nucleotide-looking sequence with `X` as `N`; supermatcher's output pairs are query-first for the sequence-showing formats and target-first for `match` / `score`.
- **Reference cross-check.** `emboss_multi_oracle.tsv` (`scripts/alignment/generate_emboss_multi_oracle.py`): 260 supermatcher + 260 needleall runs (1-3 x 1-4 sequences, 4-600 residues, every option, 12 `-aformat`s), whole output files and error files: 520 / 520 equal.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_EmbossPrograms_Tests` (3), MCP `EmbossProgramToolTests`. Commit eb4de26a.

### F125 — ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 (audit A2-29b, WP33): ksw2 extension alignment (extz / extz2_sse / extd / extd2_sse), exact cases verified; real-program oracle as Docker package
- **Finding.** No z-drop, end-bonus or extension alignment, no ambiguous-base scoring, no ksw2 CIGAR. Sources actually opened: lh3/minimap2 `ksw2.h`, `ksw2_extz2_sse.c`, `ksw2_extd2_sse.c` (v2.26 and master: the SSE files are identical, master `ksw2.h` only adds splice flags) and lh3/ksw2 `ksw2_extz.c`, `ksw2_extd.c`. **The C functions were not run:** the sandbox classifier refused to compile the downloaded source (compiling it was attempted once and not worked around); the installed `minimap2` 2.26 (apt) has no function-level interface.
- **Fix (additive).** `Ksw2Aligner.ExtendZ` / `ExtendD` (scalar, row-wise) and `ExtendZ2` / `ExtendD2` (SIMD semantics, ported lane by lane on one byte buffer with the C `mem` layout: 16-lane blocks past the band, int8 wrap-around, the unsigned / signed max / min of the SSE4.1 path, `H` in 32 bit with the 4-lane maximum and tie order), `ksw_apply_zdrop`, `ksw_backtrack` (forced states at the band edges, left / right gaps, reversed CIGAR), end bonus (`reach_end`), wildcard scoring (`mat[m*m-1]`, `-e` / `-e2` when 0), approximate max / drop; MCP `ksw2_extend`. `Ksw2Aligner` stays separate from `GlobalAlignDualAffine` (exact Gotoh over H, E1, F1, E2, F2: a different algorithm; the SIMD result is not the mathematical DP at band edges).
- **Facts established (independent program, before any C run).** The SIMD band is blurred to 16-lane blocks (a banded SIMD case is decided only by the real program); `w = 0` ends at the second anti-diagonal (`st > en`); `e2 == e` (and a second piece cheaper at k = 1) leaves the dual-gap boundary values (`long_thres`, `long_diff`) outside their domain; the scalar functions read the backtrack matrix unchecked when the corner is outside the band.
- **Reference cross-check.** `ksw2_exact_oracle.tsv` (`scripts/alignment/generate_ksw2_oracle.py`, an independent Python Gotoh program over the band, 330 cases per variant, no Z-drop / approximate flag, unbanded SIMD): max, mqe, mte, score equal and every CIGAR re-scored along its path to score / max / mqe: 1 320 / 1 320. **Docker package** `docs/Validation/review-2026-09/handoff/A2-29b-ksw2/` (Dockerfile pinned to minimap2 v2.26 + lh3/ksw2 sources with sha256 check, `harness.c`, `run.sh`, `cases.txt` = 2 000 cases incl. Z-drop, approximate flags, banded SIMD, README with the return instructions).
- **Tests.** `Ksw2Aligner_Tests` (8), MCP `EmbossProgramToolTests`. A2-29b stays DOABLE until `returned/out/ksw2_oracle.txt` is integrated (registered A2-29c).

### F126 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A2-27b, WP33): Biopython Bio.Align fasta / a2m / clustal / phylip / nexus / stockholm writers and readers; MCP `format` parameter
- **Finding.** No canonical alignment-file layer: `FormatAlignment`, `ClustalWAlignmentWriter` and `EmbossAlignmentWriter` wrote three dialects, the readers did not exist (`SequenceIO` only skips a `CLUSTAL` header). Sources actually opened: the installed Biopython 1.88 `Bio/Align/{interfaces,fasta,a2m,clustal,phylip,nexus,stockholm}.py`, `Bio/Align/__init__.py` (`parse_printed_alignment`), `Bio/Nexus/Nexus.py`, Python 3.13 `textwrap.py`.
- **Fix (additive).** `AlignmentFormats.Write` / `Format` / `Parse` (+ `BioAlignment`, `BioAlignmentRecord`, `AlignmentWriteOptions`, `AlignmentFileFormat`, `PythonTextWrap`): writers byte for byte as `Bio.Align.write` (Clustal header once, NEXUS interleave rule, Stockholm GF / GS / GR / GC with the mapping tables and `textwrap.fill`), readers as `Bio.Align.parse` (short rows padded, all-gap columns dropped, annotations as read, NEXUS `<unknown description>`). MCP: `format` (+ `names`) on `global_align`, `local_align`, `semi_global_align` and their `_affine` variants, `general_gap_align`, `stretcher_align`, `dual_affine_align`, `multiple_align`, `_anchored`, `_progressive`, `_iterative`, `_consistency`, `_clustalw`, `_tcoffee`, `_mafft` (field `formatted`; Biopython names, EMBOSS `-aformat` names, `emboss:` prefix) and `write_alignment` `dialect`.
- **Duplication that must remain (byte exactness).** Three Clustal-like writers (Biopython: 36-column names, 50-column blocks, `Biopython 1.88` header; `ClustalWAlignmentWriter`: ClustalW's program layout with numbers and the `*:.` line; EMBOSS: CLUSTAL W (1.83) layout), and the EMBOSS vs Biopython fasta / a2m / nexus / phylip writers (different widths, name rules, header lines). Each dialect is verified against its own program; merging them would change bytes. The shared pieces (`CFormat`, `MultipleAlignmentStatistics.ClustalConservationLine`) are called, not copied.
- **Reference cross-check.** `biopython_msa_oracle.tsv` (`scripts/alignment/generate_biopython_msa_oracle.py`): 1 260 written alignment sets (210 per format; 1-3 alignments, 1-8 rows, up to 1 500 columns, quoted / duplicate / empty ids, descriptions, Stockholm annotation incl. references and nested domains, nexus interleave): all byte for byte; readers equal on every file Biopython parses (a2m 210, nexus 210, stockholm 197, fasta 128, phylip 125, clustal 124; files whose alignments have different widths are excluded: Biopython's printed-alignment parser reads uninitialised memory there).
- **Not done (DOABLE, registered A2-27c).** Genomic-coordinate formats (psl, sam, bed, exonerate, mauve, chain, maf; `Alignment` with `coordinates`, strands, per-format annotations), readers msf / emboss / hhr / tabular, readers for the EMBOSS formats, T-Coffee `.score_ascii` (`t_coffee -mode evaluate`, installed), pair-markup formats of three or more rows (EMBOSS `cons`, installed as `em_cons`).
- **Tests.** `AlignmentFormats_Tests` (13), MCP `EmbossProgramToolTests`.

### F127 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A2-23, WP34): ProbCons 1.12 ported; exit code, stdout, stderr and files equal the installed program on 407 / 407 command lines
- **Finding.** No ProbCons in the library (T-Coffee's `proba_pair` embeds the pair-HMM only). Source actually opened: `probcons_1.12.orig.tar.gz` + `probcons_1.12-14.debian.tar.xz` (archive.ubuntu.com; read as text, nothing compiled or run); the oracle is the installed `probcons` 1.12-14.
- **Port (additive API).** `SequenceAligner.MultipleAlignProbCons(sequences, ProbConsOptions)` / `ProbConsRun` / `ProbConsPairs` / `ProbConsTrain` (`SequenceAligner.ProbCons.cs`), `ProbConsParameters` (built-in set, parameter files, `Describe`, `ToFileText`), `ProbConsProgram` (`Parse`, `Run`: option parser with `strtol` / `strtod`, FASTA / MSF / Clustal readers, MFA / Clustal writers, the standard-error text). **Reuse, no copy:** the pair-HMM engine of T-Coffee's `proba_pair` (`TcProbaForward`, `TcProbaBackward`, `TcProbaTotal`, `TcLookup`, `TcLogAdd`, `TcLogPlusEquals`, `TcExp`, `TcProbaTables`; `TcProbaModel` got a second constructor and `TcProbaBackward` a parameter `endMatch`: ProbCons 1.12 does not add the last cell's emission). New shared helper `CFormat.General` (`printf("%.Ng")`, NaN sign) next to `CFormat.Fixed`.
- **Quirks of the program (found in the source, confirmed by the binary, reproduced):** refinement bipartitions from glibc `rand()` at seed 1 (`GlibcRandom`); `BuildPosterior` subtracts `-co` over columns `0 .. length-1`; emission defaults for letters outside the 20 amino acids; the Debian patch message "Zero sequences delivered to function DoAlign()" with exit status 0; `-nan` parameters when training on one sequence; MSF reader quirks (gaps kept, last line without newline lost, `Name:` lines, missing-header mode).
- **Program defects not reproduced (documented):** `-go` / `-ge` fill `openingPenalty2` with swapped dimensions (heap overflow for profiles of unequal size; the oracle covers equal-size merges: two sequences, four sequences with `-ir 0` and a balanced tree), and gapped MSF / Clustal input overruns `BuildPosterior`'s mapping arrays (the oracle's alignment files are gap-free).
- **Reference cross-check.** `probcons_oracle.tsv` (scripts/alignment/generate_probcons_oracle.py): 407 command lines (every option, 21 error exits, odd FASTA, MSF / Clustal inputs, several files, training) - **407 / 407 equal** (exit code, stdout, stderr, written files).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_ProbConsOracle_Tests` (408), `SequenceAligner_ProbCons_Tests` (13), MCP `ProbConsTests` (3); T-Coffee proba tests re-run after the engine refactoring.

### F128 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A2-26, WP34): POA 2.0 (Lee et al.) ported; exit code, standard output and the PO / PIR / CLUSTAL files equal the installed program on 359 / 359 command lines
- **Finding.** No partial-order alignment in the library. Source actually opened: `poa_2.0+20060928.orig.tar.gz` + `poa_2.0+20060928-10.debian.tar.xz` (archive.ubuntu.com; read as text, nothing compiled or run); the oracle is the installed `poa` 2.0+20060928-9. Variant implemented: **POA V2 of Lee et al.** (not spoa / abPOA).
- **Port (additive API).** `SequenceAligner.MultipleAlignPoa(sequences, PoaOptions)` / `PoaRun` (`SequenceAligner.Poa.cs`, `SequenceAligner.PoaMatrix.cs`), `PoaScoreMatrix` (matrix files incl. `GAP-*` lines), `PoaProgram` (`Run`, `ReadFasta`: argument loop, FASTA reader, writers). Graph DP (`align_lpo_po`), fusion into align rings (`fuse_lpo` / `reindex_lpo_fusion`), iterative / progressive / pair-score merging (`buildup_progressive_lpo`), `-fuse_all`, `-preserve_seqorder`, heaviest bundle (`generate_lpo_bundles`), PO / PIR / CLUSTAL output, `-v`, `-printmatrix`.
- **Quirks (source + binary, reproduced):** lower-case letters outside the matrix become its first symbol; iterative merging = progressive merging along `(i, i-1)` pairs of score `min - 1`; the permutation line of `-preserve_seqorder` goes to standard output; `-hb` stops on paths shorter than 10 nodes; names are cut to 31 characters.
- **Reference cross-check.** `poa_oracle.tsv` (scripts/alignment/generate_poa_oracle.py): 359 command lines (protein BLOSUM80 + truncated / decaying gap model, nucleotides with four matrices, every option, odd FASTA, error exits) - **359 / 359 equal** (exit code, stdout, PO / PIR / CLUSTAL files). Standard error is not compared (the program prints uninitialised `%ld` arguments).

- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_PoaOracle_Tests` (360), `SequenceAligner_Poa_Tests` (9), MCP `PoaTests` (3).

### F129 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A3-1, WP35): ProbCons gap characters in the input (IndexOutOfRange) and POA non-ASCII characters (silently `A`) follow the programs
- **Defect.** `MultipleAlignProbCons(["ACGTACGT", "AC-GT"])` -> `IndexOutOfRangeException` (`Load` accepted `-`, then `BuildPosterior` indexed the mapping of a sequence whose length counts the gap); `MultipleAlignPoa(["ACG\u017fT", "AC\u0131GT"])` -> `ACGAT|ACAGT` (one `A` per non-ASCII char).
- **Source / oracle.** Installed `probcons` 1.12-14 on `>a ACGTACGT >b AC-GT` -> `ACGTACGT` / `----ACGT` (rc 0); `A.C*GT` -> `----AC*GT` ('.' dropped, '*' kept); blanks skipped; `>b -----` -> record dropped ("Alignment tree: a"). The FASTA reader (`Sequence(FileBuffer&, stripGaps)`, already ported in `ProbConsProgram.ReadSequence`) strips `-` / `.` / white space; MSF / Clustal input with gaps is undefined behaviour in the program (5 probes: Segmentation fault x2, rows of the gap-free sequences mis-aligned x3). Installed `poa` 2.0 on UTF-8 files: `ACG` U+017F `T` / `AC` U+0131 `GT` -> `ACGAAT` / `ACAAGT` (bytes, two residues per character), U+00A0 / U+0085 are two residues each (not white space).
- **Fix.** ProbCons `Load` drops `-` / `.` / white space like the reader (all-gap record: `ArgumentException` naming the sequence, because the result has one row per input); POA `MakeSequence` uses the UTF-8 byte view, ASCII-only white space and ASCII-only case conversion (`PoaByteView`, `AsciiCase`), reusing the program's `limit_residues` mapping. Digits etc. are still "Unknown character encountered" (as in the program).
- **Oracle.** `probcons_oracle.tsv` +7 rows (`gapin`: '-', '.', '*', lower case, blanks, all-gap record; 414 / 414 equal), `poa_oracle.tsv` +5 rows (`utf8`: two-byte, NBSP / NEL, `-tolower` / `-toupper`, 4-byte emoji; 364 / 364 equal; generator now writes inputs as UTF-8).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_ProbCons_Tests` +2, `SequenceAligner_Poa_Tests` +1 (rows = the installed programs).

### F130 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A3-4, WP35): empty input of the program ports follows the programs (six refuse with one message, MAFFT returns empty and writes a single sequence back)
- **Defect.** `MultipleAlignMafft([])` returned an empty result, Kalign / POA / ProbCons threw with three different texts, and ClustalW / MUSCLE / `t_coffee` returned an empty result although their programs refuse an empty file.
- **Oracle (installed programs, empty FASTA file).** `clustalw` "ERROR: No sequences in file. No alignment!"; `muscle3` "*** ERROR ***  No sequences in input file"; `t_coffee` exit 1 (format not supported); `kalign` exit 1 ("No alignment"); `poa` exit 1 ("Error reading sequence file"); `probcons` exit 1 "ERROR: No sequences read."; **`mafft` exit 0, empty output** ("Warning: Only 0 sequence found."). One sequence: `mafft` writes it back (`>a ACGT.AC` for `AC-GT.AC` with --anysymbol, lower-case `acgt.ac` plain; `AC1GT` -> `acgt`; `MKV*LA` -> `MKVLA`), `kalign` exits with "Only 1 sequence was found".
- **Fix.** One rule, one message (`SequenceAligner.EmptyInputMessage` / `RequireProgramInput`, SequenceAligner.InputContract.cs): the six refusing ports (`MultipleAlignClustalW` x3, `MultipleAlignMuscle` x3 + `MuscleRun`, `MultipleAlignTCoffeeProgram`, `MultipleAlignKalign`, `MultipleAlignPoa` / `PoaRun`, `MultipleAlignProbCons`) throw `ArgumentException("<Program> cannot align an empty list of sequences: the program refuses an empty input (<the program's own text>). MultipleAlignMafft and the Seqeron aligners (...) return an empty result.")`; `MultipleAlignMafft([])` stays empty and `MultipleAlignMafft([one])` now returns the single sequence like the program (`MafftEngine.SingleSequence`) instead of refusing it.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_InputContract_Tests` +1 (six messages, MAFFT empty), `SequenceAligner_Mafft_Tests` +7 single-sequence rows (plain / --anysymbol, = the program), seven existing tests that asserted an empty result for ClustalW / MUSCLE / t_coffee changed to the exception (library x5, MCP x3).

### F131 — ALIGN-GLOBAL/LOCAL/MULTI-001 (audit A3-2, WP35): missing resource guards / 32-bit overflow in the program ports and the format readers
- **Defect (measured, 15 GB machine).** `MultipleAlignPoa` 4 x 30 kb > 7.4 GB (`new byte[lenY * lenX]` x2 in 32-bit int: `OverflowException` above 46 k x 46 k); `MultipleAlignProbCons` `float[(L1 + 1) * (L2 + 1)]` (profile posterior, int overflow) and the pair-HMM guard worded for T-Coffee ("T-Coffee proba_pair"); `MultipleAlignMafft` 2 x 30 kb 3.5 GB / 22 s (`ijp`), 100 kb = 40 GB; `Ksw2Aligner.ExtendZ / ExtendD / ExtendZ2 / ExtendD2` traceback `byte[nCol * tlen]` unguarded, `w = int.MaxValue` -> `OverflowException`; `NeedleAlign` / `WaterAlign` limit 400 M cells = 6.4 / 3.2 GB; `Supermatcher` `float[(lena + 1) * width]` with a huge `-width`; `AlignmentFormats.Parse("999999999 999999999\nA ACGT\n", Phylip)` -> `OutOfMemoryException`; NEXUS `ntax = 99999999999` -> `OverflowException`; Stockholm `#=GF SQ` / `RN` -> raw `OverflowException`.
- **Oracle / what the programs do at their limits.** Installed needle 6.6.0 under a 4 GB address-space limit with 30 000 x 30 000: `Died: Sequences too big. Try 'stretcher'`; water: `Died: Sequences too big. Try 'matcher' or 'supermatcher'` (no fixed limit: the allocation fails); ksw2 `ksw2.h` (lh3/ksw2, fetched with the neutral User-Agent): no limit (`kmalloc`), `int8_t q, e`; Biopython `AlignIO.read("999999 999999\nA ACGT\n", "phylip")` returns 999 999 padded records (it allocates what the header says).
- **Fix.** `SequenceAligner.ResourceLimits.cs`: public constants `NeedleMaxCells` 2^27, `WaterMaxCells` 2^28, `SupermatcherMaxPathCells` 2^28, `PoaMaxMatrixCells` 2^27, `ProbConsProfileMaxCells` 2^26, `MafftMaxPairCells` 2^28, `Ksw2Aligner.MaxTracebackBytes` 2^30 and ONE exception builder (`ResourceLimit`: "&lt;program&gt; keeps ...: &lt;size&gt; exceeds the limit of N (constant). &lt;what the program does&gt;; &lt;alternative&gt;"), all in 64-bit arithmetic and raised before the allocation; the pair-HMM guard takes the program (ProbCons wording: "The ProbCons pair-HMM keeps ... ProbCons 1.12 itself ..."; T-Coffee wording unchanged); `w` wider than the matrix is clamped (no `2 * w + 1` overflow); readers: PHYLIP count vs file lines, NEXUS / Stockholm counts -> `FormatException`, Stockholm `RN` read as `long`. Reading the PHYLIP header no longer allocates per announced record.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_ResourceLimits_Tests` (13): each limit asserts the exception AND < 64 MB allocated (no large array created), the three readers, the exact-size unchanged behaviour of consistent files.

### F132 — ALIGN-GLOBAL/LOCAL-001 (audit A3-3, WP35): NaN / Infinity / overflowing penalties of the EMBOSS ports and ksw2 preconditions
- **Defect.** `NeedleAlign("ACGT", "ACT", null, NaN, 0.5)` returned score 0, `WaterAlign(..., gapExtend: NaN)` 10 (`x < 1 || x > 100` is false for NaN); same for `endOpen` / `endExtend`, `NeedleAll`, `Supermatcher`, `MatrixScoring.Emboss`; `StretcherAlign("ACGT" + A x 50, "ACT" + C x 50, null, 1e9, 1e9)` returned the POSITIVE 1 949 672 837 (32-bit wrap); `DualAffineScoring` with `int.MinValue` -> `OverflowException` (`Math.Abs(int)`); `Ksw2Aligner.ExtendZ` accepted negative `q`, `e = 0`, `w = int.MaxValue`.
- **Oracle.** Installed stretcher `-gapopen 1000000000 -gapextend 1000000000`: "Warning: stretcher CheckScore failed", Score 1949672837 (exactly the port's wrapped value; `int.MaxValue`: 1687091105); installed needle `-gapopen nan` / `-gapextend inf`: "Error: Invalid decimal value 'inf'" / `Died: needle terminated: Bad value` (the program rejects them); `ksw2.h`: `int8_t q, e`, "a gap of length l costs -(gapo + l * gape)".
- **Fix.** `RequireEmbossRange` (`!(x >= min && x <= max)`, finite) in needle / water / needleall / supermatcher (also NeedleAll up front, so an empty set no longer hides a bad penalty), `MatrixScoring.Emboss` rejects NaN / Infinity, `RequireStretcherRange` (path score < 2^28 like `PairScoring.EnsureRange`, message names "CheckScore"), `DualAffine` limit in `long`, ksw2 `CheckGapPenalties` (`q` 0..127, `e` 1..127, also `q2` / `e2`; SIMD and scalar).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_ResourceLimits_Tests`: NaN / +-Infinity on 13 parameters, stretcher 1e9 / `int.MaxValue` / 100 000 + 1e9, large exact penalties still fine, dual-affine `int.MinValue`, ksw2 preconditions, wide band equals no band.

### F133 — ALIGN-GLOBAL/LOCAL/MULTI-001 (audit A3-5, WP35): the ambient cancellation token is polled by needle, water, stretcher, supermatcher, needleall, ksw2, dual affine, T-Coffee DPs, MUSCLE refinement, anchors and distmat
- **Defect.** `AlignmentCancellation.ThrowIfRequested` appeared in the F118-F125 / F121-F124 ports only for Kalign, ProbCons, POA, MAFFT, ClustalW, TopN, LinearSpace and the T-Coffee pair-HMM; the pairwise EMBOSS ports, ksw2, dual affine, `TCoffeePrimary.cs` (Myers-Miller), `TCoffeeDp.cs` (`TcGotohProfile`), `MuscleRefine.cs`, `AnchorBasedAligner.cs` and `MultipleAlignmentStatistics.Distmat.cs` never looked at it (a 30 000 x 30 000 stretcher ran to the end after a cancel); the MCP wrappers already passed the request token, so it had no effect there.
- **Fix.** One poll per row / column / iteration boundary through the same mechanism (`SequenceAligner.Cancellation.cs`): `NeedleCore` / `WaterCore` (per column), stretcher forward and reverse sweeps (per row), `SwPathCalcFast` (per band row) + per query of `Supermatcher`, per pair of `NeedleAll`, ksw2 scalar (per target row) and SIMD (per anti-diagonal), `DualAffineCore` (per row), T-Coffee Myers-Miller (3 sweeps) and `TcGotohProfile`, `RefineHoriz` iterations and `RefineHeightParts` edges, anchor chaining and stitching, distmat pair loops; `InternalsVisibleTo Seqeron.Genomics.Analysis` (additive) lets the distmat use the ambient token. `GenomicAnalyzer.Similarity.cs` has no loop that is not O(n) hashing and no cancellation parameter: nothing to poll (not changed).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_Cancellation_Tests` +10 (a pre-cancelled token for 12 entry points; a mid-run cancel for each class that stops promptly AND whose stack trace contains the polling method: `NeedleCore`, `WaterCore`, `StretcherEngine`, `SwPathCalcFast`, `NeedleAll`, `ExtendZ` / `ExtendD` / `ExtendZ2` / `ExtendD2`, `DualAffineCore`, `MyersMillerState`, `TcGotohProfile` (reflection: the library stage would dominate an end-to-end run), `Refine`), MCP `CancellationTokenTests` (11 tools stop with a cancelled token, an uncancelled one changes nothing).

### F134 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A3-7, WP35): the true default of MUSCLE's `-maxiters` is 8; `MuscleOptions.ProgramDefaults`; ProbCons guard wording
- **Question.** `MuscleOptions.MaxIterations` = 2, "8 in the program" (AlignmentTypes.cs), installed `muscle3 -help`: "-maxiters <n> ... default 16".
- **Source + binary.** muscle3 3.8.1551-2 `params.cpp` line 109 `unsigned g_uMaxIters = 8;` (opened); the binary: on a 40-sequence 300-residue protein family `muscle3 -in x.fa` writes the rows of `-maxiters 8` and NOT those of `-maxiters 16` (`-maxiters 7` and `2` differ too); the `-help` text is stale. The 1 110 round 1-3 oracle sets already compare against `MaxIterations = 8` (their `Parse` default).
- **Decision.** The options default stays 2 (callers and tests of `MultipleAlignMuscle` depend on it: behaviour change) and the program's behaviour gets a NAME: `MuscleOptions.ProgramDefaults` (= `MaxIterations` 8; also what `MuscleRun` / `MuscleRefine` use without options); XML docs, the MCP description of `multiple_align_muscle` (`maxIters`: "pass 8 for a plain muscle -in x.fa; the -help text 'default 16' is stale") and the algorithm doc say so. The ProbCons guard message now names ProbCons (F131).
- **Oracle / tests.** `muscle3_default_iters_oracle.tsv` (2 families x {default, 8, 16, 2}, `scripts/alignment/generate_muscle3_default_iters_oracle.py`), `SequenceAligner_MuscleDefaultIterations_Tests` (11).

### F135 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A3-6, WP35): `-distance_matrix_mode random` ported (glibc `rand`, seed from `DEBUG_SRAND`); 310 / 310 sets equal; the "clock-seeded" BLOCKED proof of F109 / F111 retracted
- **Finding.** F109 / F111 recorded `random` as BLOCKED ("the program's rand is seeded differently on every run"). The source (local T-Coffee 13.41 tree, `util.c` `vsrand`): the seed is NOT the clock: `vsrand(0)` reads 4 bytes of `/dev/urandom` (`srand(pid)` when that read fails) UNLESS the environment variable `DEBUG_SRAND` is set, in which case `srand(10)`. `scripts/alignment/faketime.c` (a `time()` shim) is therefore not needed. Measured: three plain runs of the same input write three different guide trees, three runs with `DEBUG_SRAND=1` write the same tree. `seq2distance_matrix` (`util_constraints_list.c` L5184-5188) draws `id = rand() % 100`, `score = id * SCORE_K` for every pair `a < b` in row order, similarity `id`, distance `MAXID - id`.
- **Port.** `TcDistanceMatrix` case `TCoffeeDistanceMode.Random` with `GlibcRandom(seed)` (the glibc emulation of the ProbCons port, reused: no new generator) after the 19 `rand()` values that precede the first pair in the seeded stream (an `LD_PRELOAD` counter shows 20 start-up calls before the first pair, 15 of them the characters of `vtmpnamH`'s temporary-file name, one the name root, one the header tip; the first runs before `srand`; the 310 sets fit skip 19 and no other constant, found by a scan of skip 0 .. 70 per set; an LD_PRELOAD value probe confirmed that the pairs use the calls after the 20th); `TCoffeeProgramOptions.RandomSeed` (default 10 = the deterministic `DEBUG_SRAND` run; any seed reproduces a run of a program that drew it); MCP `multiple_align_tcoffee` `randomSeed`.
- **Oracle.** `generate_tcoffee_options_oracle.py` feature `dist_random` (DNA + protein, 2-8 sequences, `random` alone, with `-seq_weight t_coffee`, `-tree_mode upgma`, two methods, `-relax_lib 0`; `DEBUG_SRAND=1` set for these runs only): 310 sets appended to `tcoffee_program_options_oracle.tsv`; **310 / 310 rows equal** the port.
- **Tests.** `RandomDistance_EveryRowEqualsTheProgram_WithDebugSrand`, `RandomDistance_SeedIsAnOption_AndTheDefaultIsTheProgramsDebugSrand`; the former `NotSupportedException` assertion removed.

### F136 — GENOMIC-TANDEM-001 (audit A3-8, WP36): EMBOSS `etandem` / `equicktandem` ported exactly; 3000 / 3000 oracle rows equal
- **Finding.** F38 filed the 103 / 400 etandem differences as "etandem's own diagonal-score model" and never ported it; the reference tools of the unit were installed all along.
- **Sources actually opened.** `emboss/etandem.c`, `emboss/equicktandem.c` (EMBOSS 6.6.0, raw.githubusercontent.com/kimrutherford/EMBOSS, User-Agent `Seqeron-review/1.0`) and the installed binaries (`etandem`, `equicktandem` 6.6.0, `-origfile`).
- **Port.** `GenomicAnalyzer.FindTandemRepeatsEtandem` / `FindTandemRepeatsEquicktandem` (`GenomicAnalyzer.EmbossTandem.cs`), additive; `FindTandemRepeats` (exact runs) unchanged. Every statement of the C code is reproduced, including the head-insertion order of the candidate list (it decides ties of the final report), the duplicate-consensus removal, the 32-bit float identity, the phased wrapped consensus, the shared `Z` masking array and the terminator read of equicktandem. equicktandem refuses threshold <= 0 (the program reads an uninitialised pointer). MCP `find_tandem_repeats_emboss` (`program`, `minRepeat`, `maxRepeat`, `threshold`, `mismatch`, `uniform`).
- **Oracle.** `scripts/analysis/generate_emboss_tandem_oracle.py` -> `emboss_tandem_oracle.tsv`: 1700 etandem + 1300 equicktandem rows (random and planted arrays, substitutions / indels, N, IUPAC, lower case, -mismatch, -uniform): **3000 / 3000 rows equal** (802 / 585 non-empty, 413 / 280 multi-repeat).
- **Tests.** `GenomicAnalyzer_EmbossTandem_Oracle_Tests` (5), `Seqeron.Mcp.Analysis.Tests.EmbossProgramToolsTests` (3). Docs: Evidence / TestSpec C3-C4 / algorithm doc §5.3 / unit report / MCP docs / catalog (532 tools).
- Also fixed in passing: `SequenceAligner_Cancellation_Tests.AssertCancelsPromptly` had a `for (...; ; ...)` loop that the S1994 analyzer rejects (test project did not build at tip b77797c8); same semantics with the bound in the condition.

### F137 — ALIGN-GLOBAL/LOCAL/SEMI-001 (audit A3-10, WP36): per-direction / per-end gap scores (Biopython's twelve properties) on the existing cores
- **Finding.** `Semi_Global_Alignment.md` declared per-end penalty values "out of scope"; they are a feature of the reference tool the unit is verified against (Biopython 1.88 `PairwiseAligner`, installed) and the interior gap score could not differ by direction either.
- **Sources actually opened.** Biopython 1.88 `Bio/Align/_pairwisealigner.c` (raw.githubusercontent.com/biopython, tag biopython-188: `NEEDLEMANWUNSCH_SCORE`, `GOTOH_GLOBAL_SCORE`), the installed `PairwiseAligner` (new `open_left_insertion_score` ... and deprecated `target_*` names; target = sequence 1).
- **Implementation (additive, no second DP).** `GapScore`, `GapScoreProfile` (`FromEndGaps`, `FromBiopython`: both name families and every aggregate), `PairScoring.Gaps` (exact integer scaling incl. fractional scores) and position-dependent gap scores in `LinearFillMatrix` / `Traceback` / `AffineInitBorders` / `AffineFill` / `AffineCell` / `AffineTraceback` / local cores (a vertical run stays in one column, a horizontal run in one row: start run = column / row 0, end run = last column / row); `SequenceAligner.{Global,SemiGlobal,Local}Align[Affine](string, string, ScoringMatrix? | MatrixScoring, GapScoreProfile)`; MCP `align_gap_profile`. Legacy paths unchanged (the uniform model is a profile-less `PairScoring`). Inputs beyond the linear-space threshold are refused with a clear error.
- **Oracle.** 1000 Biopython rows (scalar, EDNAFULL, BLOSUM62, fractional gaps, global / local, linear / affine, free / equal / independently priced ends): **998 equal; 2 rows are a Biopython 1.88 linear Needleman-Wunsch corner** (`row[0] = nA * right_gap_extend_B`: a leading run consuming all of sequence 1 is charged like an end gap; its own Gotoh routine uses the left score) - an independent Python reference equals Seqeron on 1000 / 1000 and reproduces the 2 rows with the Biopython variant. All 16 `EndGaps` policies equal as profiles on 896 random pairs; independent re-scoring of every row.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_GapProfile_Tests` (9), `Seqeron.Mcp.Alignment.Tests.AlignGapProfileTests` (3); the `ScoringMatrix?` overloads carry `[OverloadResolutionPriority(1)]` (existing convention test). Docs: Semi_Global_Alignment.md §5.5 (the "out of scope" statement removed), Evidence / TestSpec GP-1..4 / unit report, MCP docs, catalog.

### F139 — GENOMIC-COMMON-001 / ALIGN-SEMI-001 / GENOMIC-REPEAT-001 (audit A3-13, WP36): EMBOSS seqmatchall and merger ported, palindrome compared
- **Finding.** The installed EMBOSS programs of the units had never been run: `seqmatchall` (all-against-all word matches), `merger` (merge two overlapping sequences), `palindrome` (B09.md had 0 hits; B04 F10 / F21 / F22 has the comparison with `RepeatFinder`, B09's facade `FindPalindromes` is a different, nested-palindrome listing).
- **Sources actually opened.** `seqmatchall.c`, `embword.c`, `merger.c`, `embaln.c` (`embAlignPathCalc`, `embAlignWalkNWMatrix`), `palindrome.c` earlier by B04 (EMBOSS 6.6.0, raw.githubusercontent.com/kimrutherford/EMBOSS, User-Agent `Seqeron-review/1.0`), the installed binaries; `apt-get install mash` for A3-11.
- **seqmatchall.** `GenomicAnalyzer.SeqMatchAll` + MCP `seqmatchall`: maximal exact matches of every pair via the canonical suffix-tree engine, and the program's `embWordGetTable` scan (it inspects only the symbol entering a window, so a window restarted behind a skipped N can still hold an N) for sequences with the unknown symbol. 330 / 330 oracle sets equal (700+ pairs; nucleotide, N, lower case, protein).
- **merger.** `SequenceAligner.Merger` + MCP `merger`: `embAlignPathCalc` / `embAlignWalkNWMatrix` and `merger_Merge` ported (the walk is NOT needle's compass traceback; the "overlap alignment + consensus" hypothesis is refuted). 450 / 450 oracle rows equal (merged sequence, conflicts, score). The program's report warnings are unreachable.
- **palindrome.** `FindPalindromes(2m, 2M)` per-centre longest = `palindrome -gaplimit 0 -nummismatches 0 -overlap` on 330 / 330 sequences; documented (Palindrome_Detection.md).
- **Registered (precise sub-items, not done here):** A3-13b `megamerger` (merger of two large sequences by word matching: `megamerger.c` + `embWordMatchMin` / `wordmatch`, installed; depends on the A2-28d `wordmatch` port), A3-13c `showalign` (display of an alignment set with options), A3-13d `consambig` (ambiguity-code consensus of an alignment).
- **Tests.** `GenomicAnalyzer_EmbossPrograms_Oracle_Tests` (4), `SequenceAligner_Merger_Oracle_Tests` (2), MCP `EmbossProgramToolsTests` (+1), `MergerTests` (1).

### F140 — GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001 (audit A3-12, WP36): fuzzpro and fuzztran ported (900 + 760 oracle rows); the fuzznuc sequence-ambiguity rule measured (A3-12a registered)
- **Finding.** The protein / translated siblings of the ported fuzznuc pattern language were missing; the doc's "fuzznuc reads a sequence ambiguity code as a base set" was unverified.
- **Sources actually opened.** `fuzzpro.c`, `fuzztran.c`, `fuzznuc.c`, `embpat.c` (EMBOSS 6.6.0, raw.githubusercontent.com/kimrutherford/EMBOSS), installed `fuzzpro` / `fuzztran` / `fuzznuc` / `transeq` 6.6.0 (`-help`: neither protein program has `-pmismatch`), `ajtranslate.c` (`trnComplete`).
- **Implementation.** `ProteinPattern` (grammar, B / Z / J, x, class / exclusion / repeat / range / anchors), `GenomicAnalyzer.FindProteinPatternHits(string | ProteinPattern)` (exact; every window; start-anchored range = longest only; one trailing `*` read off) and `FindTranslatedPatternHits(nucleotides, pattern, frames, table)` over the canonical `Translator.TranslateSixFrames(EmbossTranseq)` (complete codons only, forward-strand location, program coordinates for the forward frames); MCP `fuzzpro`, `fuzztran`.
- **Oracle.** fuzzpro **900 / 900** (57 rejected patterns, 275 with hits, 456 ranges, 263 anchored, 130 with B / Z / J); fuzztran **760 / 760** (frames 1 2 3 F -1 -2 -3 R 6, tables 0-11; 384 with hits). Measured on the binaries: B / Z / J letters, start-anchored range = longest, trailing `*`, complete codons only, reverse-frame coordinates meaningless (not reproduced), EMBOSS ambiguous-codon table differs from the Biopython-style one for 13 / 3375 codons (B02 limitation, N-only oracle).
- **Registered (A3-12a, precise).** fuzznuc with an IUPAC code IN THE SEQUENCE: measured rule = letters compared literally; a pattern IUPAC letter stands for its bases and itself; `N` any symbol; an exclusion any symbol not listed; a class the listed bases only. Needs a letter / class / exclusion kind in `PatternElement`, set expansion instead of IUPAC strings, reverse strand through the IUPAC complement of the sequence, a `sequenceAmbiguity` option on `FindPatternHits` / MCP `find_pattern_hits`, oracle = `generate_fuzznuc_pattern_oracle.py` with IUPAC sequences (>= 1 000 rows).

### F144 — test-tier memory and robustness (audit A3-15, WP37): heavy fixtures run alone, oracle parsed once per fixture, the 20 s timing flake removed
- **Finding.** `Parallelization.cs` runs fixtures 8-way on a 4-core / 16 GB container; the tier once ended in an OOM kill and once in the flaky `CancelledWhileRunning_GenomicAnalyzerScans_StopPromptly` ("did not stop within the limit": the cancelled call waits for the uninterruptible suffix-tree build of 3 M bases, `DnaSequence.SuffixTree`, while seven other workers compete).
- **Measurement (LOCAL_RUN.md section 5, per fixture, peak working set of the process tree, runtime baseline ~270 MB).** `SequenceAligner_Cancellation_Tests` 1.9-2.5 GB, `SequenceAligner_LinearSpace_Tests` 1.8-2.2 GB, `GenomicAnalyzer_FindOpenReadingFrames_Oracle_Tests` 0.9 GB, `SequenceAligner_TopNLinearSpace_Tests` 0.6-0.7 GB, `SequenceAligner_TCoffeeOptions_Tests` 0.9 GB (-> 0.54 GB), `GenomicAnalyzer_FindCommonRegion_Tests` 0.5 GB; the 31 other measured fixtures 0.27-0.46 GB. Eight of the big ones at once are > 10 GB.
- **Changes.** `[NonParallelizable]` on the five fixtures above 0.5 GB with the measured reason in a comment, and the rule in `Parallelization.cs`; `SequenceAligner_TCoffeeOptions_Tests.Oracle()` / `SequenceAligner_TCoffeeExternal_Tests.Oracle()` re-read and re-parsed the 6.0 / 3.2 MB embedded TSV in every test (881 MB -> 537 MB peak, 484 -> 401 MB), twelve `GenomicAnalyzer_*` oracle fixtures called `ReadOracle()` two to six times: all parse once (`Lazy<>`); the two tree-building calls of the genomic cancellation test have a 120 s limit (the build is uninterruptible by design) and the fixture runs alone.
- **Tier time.** The auditor's filter (`SequenceAligner|AlignmentFormats|GenomicAnalyzer|Ksw2|Emboss|MultipleAlignmentStatistics|CFormat`, fast-tier exclusions): before 16 m 46 s (auditor, 6 547 tests, culture run); after 19 m 36 s, 6 640 / 0 (this container, default culture, +93 tests; the five fixtures now run one after the other: about 2-3 minutes of the wall time). A before-run on the same state was not repeated.
- **Embedded test data (decision).** Plain TSV kept: `TestData/` 76 MB in 133 files (largest `tcoffee_program_options_oracle` 6.0, `emboss_aformat_oracle` 5.4, `orffinder_cli_oracle` 4.7 MB), hand-off packages 21 MB, `.git` 210 MB (pack 91 MiB, already deflated: `gzip -9` of the three largest = 4x / 16x / 4x, the same ratio); `.gz` resources would not shrink the repository, defeat delta compression when regenerated, touch 94 inline readers and remove row-by-row `git diff` / `grep` review of the oracles.

### F146 — GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001 (audit A3-12a, WP38): fuzznuc with an IUPAC code in the SEQUENCE implemented (subset rule, 1 350 rows)
- **Finding.** The sequence side of `FindPatternHits(string ...)` was literal (an ambiguity code matched nothing, not even `N`); the binary does something else (F140 measured part of it, wrongly summarised as "letter matches itself and its bases").
- **Sources actually opened.** installed `fuzznuc` 6.6.0 (the oracle; `-pmismatch`, `-complement`, `-rformat gff`); `embpat.c` earlier (F18-F20, F140).
- **Rule (measured, 1 350 rows, 0 disagreements; the first hypothesis failed 59 / 450).** Pattern letter L matches symbol S iff bases(S) within bases(L) (R matches A, G, R; sequence R matches the letters R, V, D, N); `N` any symbol (X included); class = listed plain bases only; exclusion = every symbol not listed (`{ACGT}` = non-A/C/G/T symbols); U reads as T; `-` is removed by the reader (outside the contract); reverse strand = IUPAC reverse complement of the sequence searched with the same pattern.
- **Implementation.** `PatternElementKind` on `PatternElement`; `FuzznucPattern.ExpandPaths` / `Matches`; `ExpandWithExtras` derived from `ExpandPaths` (plain expansion unchanged, 3 300 original rows still equal); `GenomicAnalyzer.FindPatternHits(string ..., sequenceAmbiguity)` (3 string overloads); MCP `find_pattern_hits` `sequenceAmbiguity`.
- **Tests.** `GenomicAnalyzer_PatternHits_IupacSequence_Tests` (5), MCP `StrandPatternAndRepeatToolsTests` (+1).

### F150 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A3-9a, WP38): nucmer's mgaps clustering ported (758 + 700 oracle runs); AnchorBasedAligner compared
- **Finding.** `AnchorBasedAligner` ("MUMmer-inspired") had never been compared with `nucmer`; the first stage that differs is the clustering of the anchors.
- **Sources actually opened.** mummer4 `src/tigr/mgaps.cc` + `include/mummer/mgaps.hh` (the exact 3.23 sources are not reachable here; the algorithm is unchanged), the installed `nucmer` 3.23 script (the pipeline and its default `-g 90 -c 65 -D 5 -d 0.12 -b 200`), the installed `mummer` / `mgaps` binaries.
- **Implementation.** `NucmerClusters.Cluster` (Alignment, public, `NucmerClusterOptions`) = `Cluster_each` / `Filter_Matches` / `Process_Cluster` / `Print_Cluster` line by line; `GenomicAnalyzer.FindNucmerClusters` (the three `mummer` match modes of A2-30, both strands, reverse coordinates as mummer prints them); MCP `nucmer_clusters`.
- **Oracle (behavioural: the program's input / output, since only the binary of 3.23 is available).** `mgaps_oracle.tsv`: 758 / 758 match lists (mummer `-maxmatch` outputs of related pairs + shuffled synthetic lists, all of `-l -s -d -f -e`); `nucmer_clusters_oracle.tsv`: 700 / 700 related pairs through `mummer -mum | -mumreference | -maxmatch -b | mgaps` (288 with reverse-strand clusters).
- **Semantic differences to `AnchorBasedAligner` (documented, Evidence ALIGN-MULTI-001).** One MEM per diagonal run + maximum-weight chain with no distance / diagonal limit + Needleman-Wunsch in EVERY gap, versus MUMs joined by diagonal difference and separation, short clusters dropped, banded Smith-Waterman extension. No defect found in `AnchorBasedAligner`.
- **Tests.** `NucmerClusters_Tests` (3), `GenomicAnalyzer_NucmerClusters_Tests` (2), MCP `EmbossProgramToolsTests` (+1) / registration.
- **Registered, not done here (precise).** A3-9b: `postnuc` (cluster extension: `extendClustersForward/Backward`, `-b` break length, banded SW `-B`, delta output) + `delta-filter -g/-1/-q/-r` + `show-aligns` / `show-coords` rows, oracle = `nucmer` delta files; A3-9c: minimap2 2.26 chaining (see the worklist).

### F153 — ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 / ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / ALIGN-SEMI-001 / ALIGN-STATS-001 / GENOMIC-COMMON-001 / GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001 / GENOMIC-TANDEM-001 / MOTIF-CONS-001 (audit A4-3, WP39): the EMBOSS reader drops gap characters and every other non-letter from plain input sequences - one shared cleaner for all ports
- **Defect.** EMBOSS 6.6.0 removes every character that is not a letter or `*` from a plain input sequence BEFORE any length check, coordinate or score; no Seqeron port did. Minimal repro: `needle AC-GTTAGC.TT* ACGTTAGCTT` prints `# Length: 11`, `# Score: 58.0`; `SequenceAligner.NeedleAlign("AC-GTTAGC.TT*", "ACGTTAGCTT")` scored 43 (gap characters kept and scored); `WaterAlign` 43, `StretcherAlign` 27, `Merger` kept `.` and `-` in the merged sequence, `DotMatcher.Compute` scored a gap 0 and kept its position (68 of 300 random gapped pairs differed), `FindTandemRepeatsEtandem("ACACAC-ACACAC.ACAC")` counted the gap characters, `FindPatternHits` / `FindProteinPatternHits` / `FindTranslatedPatternHits` / `Supermatcher` / `NeedleAll` / matcher (`TopNTieOrder.Sim`) / `SeqMatchAll` / `MegaMerge` / `FindTandemRepeatsEquicktandem` likewise; `consambig` / `showalign` threw "Invalid alignment character" for a digit or a blank (the program drops them) and read `#` / `+` as invalid.
- **Source (what was actually opened).** EMBOSS `ajax/core/ajseqread.c` (`seqAppend`: `ajStrKeepSetAlphaC(.., "*.~?#+-")`), `ajseqtype.c` (`ajSeqTypeCheckIn` / `seqTypeFix`: an UNGAPPED type calls `ajStrRemoveGap`; the `seqType[]` table), `ajstr.c` (`ajStrRemoveGap` keeps exactly `A-Za-z*`), `ajseq.c` (`ajSeqIsNuc`, `ajSeqGapStandard`) from raw.githubusercontent.com/kimrutherford/EMBOSS (User-Agent `Seqeron-review/1.0`); the installed ACD files (`/usr/share/EMBOSS/acd/<program>.acd`: `type:` of each sequence input is `any` / `dna` / `nucleotide` / `protein` / `stopprotein`, all ungapped; `gapany` for consambig / showalign); the installed programs.
- **Measurement.** `scripts/alignment/measure_emboss_reader_chars.py`: each character 1..255 except newline / carriage return / `>` inserted into the middle of a sequence, run through needle, water, stretcher, matcher, merger, megamerger, seqmatchall, fuzznuc, fuzzpro, fuzztran, etandem, equicktandem, dotmatcher, palindrome (both sequences, nucleotide and protein; the output with the character equals the output without it): **199 characters are dropped by every program** (everything except the 52 ASCII letters and `*`; `*` is kept, a protein stop, an error in a nucleotide-typed input). Alignment readers (consambig, showalign; `gapany`): `. ~ -` gaps, `? # +` the unknown residue (`ACGT#CGT`, `ACGT+CGT`, `ACGT?CGT` give the same consensus and set type), every other non-letter dropped, rows padded after cleaning (non-ASCII bytes depend on the C library's `isalpha` and the locale: dropped here, not part of the oracle). A sequence that is empty afterwards: "Unable to read sequence" (zero length).
- **Fix.** `EmbossSequenceReader` (Infrastructure, public, one file): `Clean` (letters and `*`), `CleanGapped` (alignment rows), `IsNucleotide` (the single `ajSeqIsNuc` rule: it replaces `DotMatcher.IsNucleotide`, `MotifFinder.IsEmbossNucleotideSequence` + `EmbossNucGapChars`, `StretcherAlign.IsNucleotideText`, `PairWordMatcher`'s `IsNucleotideWordSequence`, `EmbossAlignmentWriter.IsNuc`), `Clean(IReadOnlyList<EmbossSequence>)`. Called by every EMBOSS-named entry point: `NeedleAlign`, `WaterAlign`, `StretcherAlign`, `Merger`, `NeedleAll`, `Supermatcher`, `LocalAlign(Affine)TopN(.., TopNTieOrder.Sim)`, `DotMatcher.Compute`, `MegaMerge`, `SeqMatchAll`, `FindPatternHits` (string overloads), `FindProteinPatternHits`, `FindTranslatedPatternHits`, `FindTandemRepeatsEtandem`, `FindTandemRepeatsEquicktandem`, `GenerateEmbossConsensus`, `GenerateEmbossAmbiguousConsensus`, `ShowAlign`, `CalculateDistanceMatrix` (distmat). The raw route stays: the non-EMBOSS-named entry points (`GlobalAlign`, `LocalAlign`, `FindMotif`, the `DnaSequence` overloads, ...) keep every character literally (no caller of this repository needed a literal route into an EMBOSS-named entry, so no option was added). MCP descriptions of the 17 tools and `docs/mcp` regenerated (`gen-catalog.py --check` fresh, 548 tools).
- **Oracle.** `emboss_gap_oracle.tsv` (`generate_emboss_gap_oracle.py`, seed 20261126): 18 programs (the 17 plus distmat) x 240 rows = 4 320 rows whose inputs contain `- . ~ ? # + _`, digits, blanks, quotes, brackets, `*` (protein), lower case and non-ASCII letters; **4 320 / 4 320 equal** (needle / water / stretcher score + rows, matcher alignment lists, merger / megamerger merged sequence, seqmatchall match lists, supermatcher / needleall rows, fuzznuc / fuzzpro / fuzztran hits + the GFF header's sequence length, etandem / equicktandem reports, dotmatcher plots, consambig consensus, showalign text, distmat matrices). Needle scores 58 (Length 11) where the program does.
- **Tests.** `EmbossGapCharacters_Oracle_Tests` (18 oracle tests, the 58 example, the 256-code-point table of `Clean`, `CleanGapped`, `IsNucleotide`).
- **Not changed (cross-batch note).** `palindrome` reads its sequence the same way (measured: the same 199 characters); `RepeatFinder` (another batch's file) has no EMBOSS-named string entry, so nothing to switch. `distmat` (`MultipleAlignmentStatistics.CalculateDistanceMatrix`, `gapany` like consambig) uses `CleanGapped` and reads `? # +` as X (N in a nucleotide row) as well: 240 oracle rows (all `-nucmethod` / `-protmethod` values) equal.
- **Commit.** see the worklist tick of A4-3.

### F154 — ALIGN-STATS-001 (audit A4-5 and A4-2 (b), WP39): `ShowAlign` allocated O(L^2 / width) and overflowed at the extremes of `Width` / `Margin`
- **Defect.** `OutputSeq` built `new string(seq)` (a copy of the WHOLE row) for every block of every row: width 1 over two 100 kb rows = 6.2 MB of output, 60 GB allocated, 12 s; `Margin = int.MaxValue` -> raw `OutOfMemoryException: Array dimensions exceeded`; `Width = int.MaxValue` took 9.4 s for a 9-column alignment; `int iend = pos + width + margin - 1` overflowed 32 bit.
- **Fix.** `OutputSeq` appends the slice of the `char[]` (`StringBuilder.Append(char[], first, count)`, the `EmbossText.Substring` clipping kept), the upper-case ranges are only looked at when there are any; `Width` is clamped to the alignment length (the text of one block is the same: `pos + width + margin - 1` reaches the end either way), the position arithmetic is 64 bit, and the display text is bounded by the documented `MultipleAlignmentDisplay.ShowAlignMaxOutputCharacters` (2^27 characters; blocks x lines x (margin + width + 1) computed in 64 bit BEFORE the rows are normalised or anything is built): a larger request is an `ArgumentException` of the `SequenceAligner.ResourceLimit` style (the program streams its output and has no such limit). The dead local `char[] r` / `_ = r;` of `Output` is gone.
- **Oracle.** `showalign_oracle.jsonl` 600 / 600 equal (unchanged); `emboss_gap_oracle.tsv` showalign 240 / 240.
- **Tests.** `MultipleAlignmentDisplay_ShowAlign_Tests`: `Width_BeyondTheAlignment_...` (the text equals the text of the alignment width, instantly), `Margin_OrWidth_AboveTheOutputLimit_IsRefusedBeforeAnythingIsBuilt` (exception text, < 64 MB allocated, 40-million-column case, the last margin that fits is accepted), `Output_AllocationIsLinearInTheText_NotQuadratic` (width 1 over 2 x 20 000 columns: < 120 MB; 1.6 GB before).

### F155 — GENOMIC-COMMON-001 (audit A4-6, WP39): `MegaMerge` `MinimalMatchSet` without the O(k) list shifts - and the measured cost of the 20 kb poly-A case is NOT this loop
- **Finding.** `MinimalMatchSet` shifted the list tail for every `RemoveAt(0)` / `RemoveAt(i)` and sorted the whole list again after every truncation. Fixed: the head is an index, one pass compacts the survivors in place, the shortened matches (new keys) are sorted on their own and merged into the sorted survivors; same result, same `InvalidOperationException`s in the same order (every remaining match is still examined against every kept one: the program's loop, O(k) per kept match).
- **Measurement (against the audit's attribution).** `SeqMatchAll` of two poly-A sequences (no `MinimalMatchSet` at all) takes 2.85 s / 10.8 s for 4 000 / 8 000 residues and `MegaMerge` 2.94 s / 10.85 s: the 69 s of the 20 kb case are the maximal-match enumeration of the shared suffix-tree engine (`SuffixTreeAlgorithms.FindMaximalExactMatches`: for every query position all text occurrences of the longest match are collected before the left-maximal ones are kept, O(n x m) for repeats), not the minimal-match loop (60 000 matches: 0.2 s after the change, `List.RemoveAt` shifts about 1-2 s before). The installed `megamerger` is quadratic as well: 1 500 poly-A residues take it 20 s, 3 000 more than 100 s. Cross-batch request (B05, `SuffixTree/Algorithms/SuffixTree.Core/SuffixTreeAlgorithms.cs`): collect only left-maximal occurrences (a left-character summary per node makes the enumeration output-sensitive); registered below.
- **Tests.** `GenomicAnalyzer_MegaMerge_Tests`: `MinimalMatchSet_EqualsTheListShiftingOriginal_OnRandomRepetitiveMatchLists_ExceptionsIncluded` (400 repetitive match lists: the original loop is kept in the test as the reference, results and aborts equal), `MinimalMatchSet_OfSixtyThousandDiagonalMatches_IsFast`, `PolyA_IsTheProgramsResult_ForTheLengthsTheProgramFinishes` (600 / 1 500: megamerger 6.6.0 prints the whole sequence, "Matching region a 1-n : b 1-n"), `TandemRepeat_MegaMerge_IsTheProgramsResult_AndCancellable`. The 600 / 600 megamerger oracle is unchanged.

### F156 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 / GENOMIC-TANDEM-001 (audit A4-4, WP39): option boundaries of the minimap2 chaining, mgaps clustering and etandem ports measured on the programs
- **Finding.** The audit listed `MinimapChainOptions` (`KmerSize = 0`, `Bandwidth = -5`, `MaxChainIterations = 0`, ... `ChainGapScale = NaN`), `NucmerClusterOptions` (negative / NaN values) and `etandem -threshold int.MinValue` (`DivideByZeroException` "while the program returns 0 hits") as accepted-but-wrong. Measured on the installed programs (minimap2 2.26, mgaps 3.23, etandem 6.6.0; `generate_option_boundary_oracle.py`, 30 + 17 + 12 rows): **the programs accept almost all of these values**; the only rejections are `mm_check_opt` "-k and -w must be positive" (k <= 0) and the `mm_sketch` assertion k <= 28; mgaps rejects nothing (negative, zero, NaN, infinite values run). The `etandem` claim was wrong in the other direction: the program does NOT return 0 hits for `-threshold -2147483648` - it dies with SIGFPE ("Floating point exception", exit status 136; `etandem.c`: `reportRoot->bestScore = thresh - 1` wraps to INT_MAX, the report loop takes the root (repeat size 0) as the top entry and divides by it) whenever one repeat qualifies, and prints nothing when none does (`AAAAAAAA`, `A`); the port's `DivideByZeroException` was the same death.
- **Source (opened).** minimap2 v2.26 `options.c` (`mm_check_opt`), `sketch.c` (assertion), `lchain.c` (`comput_sc`: `sc -= (int)(lin_pen + .5f * log_pen)`), `etandem.c` (`etandem_report`, `etandem_finalReport`, `thresh - 1` at the report root) from raw.githubusercontent.com (User-Agent `Seqeron-review/1.0`); the installed programs on the boundary values.
- **Fix.** (1) `MinimapChainOptions.KmerSize` outside 1 - 28 is an `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` (message quotes the program); every other value is accepted with the program's arithmetic - and the C float-to-int conversion of a NaN / infinite / huge penalty (`cvttss2si` gives INT_MIN; .NET gives 0 / saturates) is reproduced (`CInt`, unchecked subtraction), so `ChainGapScale` = NaN / Infinity / 1e30 give the program's chains. (2) `NucmerClusterOptions`: every value accepted; the `(int)(f x separation)` of the port became the program's `(long)` conversion (`CLong`: NaN / beyond-64-bit = LONG_MIN), so `-f nan / inf / 1e30` (no fraction added) and `-f 1e10` (everything joins) equal mgaps. (3) `FindTandemRepeatsEtandem`: `threshold - 1` is explicitly unchecked and the program's SIGFPE is an `InvalidOperationException` that says so (raised exactly when the program would die: threshold = int.MinValue and a repeat qualifies; otherwise the empty list). (4) `CollectAnchors(queryOccurrenceFraction: NaN)`: the program accepts it (`q_occ_frac > 0` is false: filter off; -1 and inf too: measured), the port already equalled it - locked by a test. The XML docs state each rule.
- **Tests.** `OptionBoundaries_Oracle_Tests` (5): 30 minimap2 option values (chain score + anchor count of every hit equal to the program's PAF `s1` / `cm`; the oracle runs use `-r 500,500`: the long-join bandwidth is the registered A4-7), 17 mgaps option values (clusters equal), `KmerSize` 0 / -3 / 29 / 40 / int.MinValue / int.MaxValue refused, the `q_occ_frac` values, 12 etandem rows (5 crash rows throw, 2 empty rows at int.MinValue, int.MinValue + 1 and above exact).
- **MCP.** `minimap2_chains`, `nucmer_clusters`, `find_tandem_repeats_emboss` descriptions state the rules (catalog `--check` fresh, 548 tools).

### F157 — ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / ALIGN-MULTI-001 / ALIGN-SEMI-001 / ALIGN-STATS-001 / GENOMIC-COMMON-001 / GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001 / MOTIF-CONS-001 (audit A4-1, WP39): the ports added in WP36 - WP38 poll the ambient cancellation token; six MCP tools take a token
- **Defect.** `AlignmentCancellation.Poll` / `ThrowIfRequested` count = 0 in `DotMatcher`, `Minimap2Chaining`, `NucmerClusters`, `SequenceAligner.Merger`, `SequenceAligner.TopNSim`; `GenomicAnalyzer.MegaMerge` polled before / after the match listing only; `FindTranslatedPatternHits`, `ShowAlign`, `GenerateEmbossAmbiguousConsensus` took no token; `FindNucmerClusters` passed its token to `FindCommonRegions` and never into `NucmerClusters.Cluster`. Measured: `DotMatcher.Compute` 3 000 x 3 000 = 0.5 s (100 kb x 100 kb about 10 min), `NucmerClusters.Cluster` of 300 000 collinear matches (one cluster: the program's own O(c^2) chain DP) did not return in > 2 min, `Minimap2Chaining` of a 57 kb tandem repeat 10.8 s. MCP `dotmatcher`, `minimap2_chains`, `merger`, `fuzztran`, `showalign`, `consambig` had no `CancellationToken` parameter.
- **Fix (the existing ambient mechanism, `SequenceAligner.Cancellation.cs`).** New trailing `CancellationToken cancellationToken = default` (entering the scope) on `DotMatcher.Compute`, `Minimap2Chaining.Chain` / `CollectAnchors` / `MapChains`, `NucmerClusters.Cluster`, `SequenceAligner.Merger`, `GenomicAnalyzer.FindTranslatedPatternHits`, `MotifFinder.GenerateEmbossAmbiguousConsensus`, and `ShowAlignOptions.CancellationToken`; `FindNucmerClusters` passes its token into `Cluster`; `MegaMerge` passes its token into `MinimalMatchSet`. Polls: one per diagonal (`DotMatcher`), every 64 anchors / 256 chains (`Chain`), every 65 536 k-mer positions and per 256 query minimizers (`CollectAnchors` / `Sketch`), per match of the filter / union / DP passes (`NucmerClusters`), per path-matrix column and every 1 024 walk steps (`Merger`), per row of the big / small / diff passes and per reported alignment (`TopNSim`), per kept match (`MinimalMatchSet`), per frame and every 65 536 residues (`ProteinPatternHitsCore`: also `fuzzpro`), per row / 1 024 columns of the consensus and per block of the display (`ShowAlign`), per row and 8 192 columns (`consambig`). MCP: the six tools take the token and hand it to the library (`BeginCancellationScope` / the library parameter).
- **Not interruptible (stated).** The maximal-match enumeration of the shared suffix-tree engine (`SuffixTreeAlgorithms.FindMaximalExactMatches`, B05) runs to completion before `MegaMerge` / `SeqMatchAll` / `FindCommonRegions` see its list; it is the part of a poly-A `MegaMerge` that takes minutes (F155).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_Cancellation_Tests` (+13): `PreCancelled_A4_1_EveryPortAddedInWp36ToWp38_Throws` (11 entry points) and one mid-run cancel test per class (the `AssertCancelsPromptly` harness, the observing poll named: `Diagonal`, `Chain`, `CollectAnchors`, `ProcessCluster`, `MergerPathCalc`, `SimMatcher`, `MinimalMatchSet`, `FindTranslatedPatternHits`, `ProteinPatternHitsCore`, `Output`, `EmbossConsensusCore`, `GenerateEmbossAmbiguousConsensus`); MCP `CancellationTokenTests` (dotmatcher / minimap2_chains / merger) and `EmbossProgramToolsTests` (fuzztran / showalign / consambig).

### F158 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 / ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / GENOMIC-COMMON-001 (audit A4-2, WP39): resource guards for the big allocations of the new ports (the ShowAlign part is F154)
- **Defect.** Measured: `Minimap2Chaining.CollectAnchors(polyA100k, polyA100k)` -> `OutOfMemoryException` after 10 s (every position is a minimizer, `mid_occ` becomes 100 001: 10^10 anchors); a 57 kb tandem repeat against itself 44 976 004 anchors, 5.2 GB allocated in 10.8 s (a `MinimapAnchor` is 20 bytes, the `List` doubles); `LocalAlignAffineTopN(.., maxAlignments: int.MaxValue, TopNTieOrder.Sim)` -> `OutOfMemoryException` at `new Vertex[k]` (the Waterman-Eggert order accepts it: 23 alignments); `MegaMerge` / `SeqMatchAll` / `DotMatcher.Compute` / `NucmerClusters.Cluster` materialise every match / segment / cluster with no bound.
- **Fix.** Documented public limits in `SequenceAligner.ResourceLimits.cs`, all raised as the one `SequenceAligner.ResourceLimit` `ArgumentException` BEFORE the allocation in 64-bit arithmetic: `MinimapMaxAnchors` (2^23: the sum of the occurrences of the query minimizers is counted before any anchor is stored; `Chain` refuses a longer list before it reads one; minimap2 itself has no such limit and drops frequent minimizers: the registered A4-7), `DotMatcherMaxSegments` (2^22, checked as the list grows), `NucmerMaxClusterMatches` (100 000 matches of ONE cluster: the program's O(c^2) chain DP, checked before the DP), `WordMatchMaxMatches` (2^22 matches of a pair; the engine's own list is checked as soon as the shared suffix-tree enumerator returns it - that engine is not bounded nor interruptible, F155 - and `WordTableMatches` while it builds), `TopNSimMaxVertices` (2^22). `TopNTieOrder.Sim`: the vertex list is allocated lazily (`VertexAt`), so `maxAlignments = int.MaxValue` means "every alignment" exactly as in the Waterman-Eggert order (the program calls `calloc(K)` up front and dies for such a K); the bound turns an endless list into the clear exception. `ShowAlign` Margin / Width: F154. The sequence-length limit of the 31-bit minimizer positions needs no check (a .NET string is at most 2^30 characters).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_ResourceLimits_Tests` (+7, each asserts the exception text, the documented constant and a low allocation): `Minimap2CollectAnchors_PolyAAndTandemQueries_...` (100 kb poly-A, 60 kb period-2 tandem, an ordinary pair untouched), `Minimap2Chain_AboveTheAnchorLimit_...` (a list that throws when read), `TopNSim_MaxAlignmentsIntMaxValue_MeansEveryAlignment_...` (equal to the 100 000 run, both orders and both overloads accept it), `TopNSim_VertexListIsBounded_...`, `WordMatches_AboveTheMatchLimit_...` (5.1 million matches, seqmatchall and megamerger), `DotMatcher_SegmentList_IsBounded_...` (6.75 million segments), `NucmerCluster_OfMoreThan100000Matches_...`.

### F159 — ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 / ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / ALIGN-MULTI-001 / MOTIF-PATTERNS (audit A4-15, WP40): the limit / guard exception messages format numbers with the invariant culture
- **Defect.** `SequenceAligner.Merger.cs:70` (`{a.Length:N0} x {b.Length:N0} residues`), `ResourceLimits.cs` `ResourceLimit` (`{limit:N0}`) and 17 more interpolated `N0` literals of Alignment / Analysis files (needle / water / supermatcher / POA / MAFFT / MUSCLE / ProbCons / T-Coffee / ksw2 / general-gap / the fuzznuc and fuzzpro repeat limit) used the CURRENT culture (`12,000` / `12 000` / `12.000`); the results (not the messages) were culture-free.
- **Source.** The .NET `FormattableString` / `string.Create(IFormatProvider, ...)` contract; no external tool.
- **Fix.** 19 literals wrapped in `string.Create(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, $"...")` (the analyzer S6618 forbids `FormattableString.Invariant`). **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_ResourceLimits_Tests.LimitMessages_AreCultureInvariant` (needle, merger, POA messages under a culture with `.` thousands and `,` decimal separators). **Commit.** 90de81b5.

### F163 — ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 / ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / ALIGN-SEMI-001 (audit A4-8, WP40): `GapScoreProfile` alignments above the full-matrix limit
- **Defect.** `SequenceAligner.GapProfile.cs:21` / `RequireFullMatrixForGapProfile`: a profile on an input above `LinearSpaceCellThreshold` (25 M cells) was refused ("the linear-space program has one gap model").
- **Source.** Hirschberg 1975 / Myers & Miller 1988 (the existing F46 cores, the state-carrying midpoint), Biopython 1.88 `PairwiseAligner` (scores). No new third-party source: the recursion is the existing one with position-dependent gap scores.
- **Fix.** `PairScoring.Window(rowOffset, colOffset, totalRows, totalCols)` (a sub-rectangle resolves start / interior / end gap scores by its position in the WHOLE matrix) and `InteriorOnly()` (local: interior scores only, direction-specific); `LinearMidpoint` / `AffineMidpoint` / `LocateLinearLocal` / `LocateAffine` take per-column (vertical) and per-row (horizontal) gap scores; the three refusals removed; 12 forceable overloads `GlobalAlign[Affine]LinearSpace` / `SemiGlobalAlign[Affine]LinearSpace` / `LocalAlign[Affine]LinearSpace` with a `GapScoreProfile` (scalar and `MatrixScoring`, `maxFullMatrixCells`). MCP `align_gap_profile` description / docs updated (the error row removed).
- **Verification.** 600 forced-recursion cases (6 methods x scalar / BLOSUM62 fractional, random profiles with free / interior / priced members, base-case sizes 1, 2, 9, 40, 300) row-for-row equal to the full matrix; the 1 000 Biopython oracle rows with base size 1 equal the full-matrix rows and the Biopython score; an 8 700 x 7 999 pair (69.6 M cells, automatic switch): Biopython scores 12 242 (global and local affine) / 13 824 (local linear = an independent numpy DP of the global linear scheme; Biopython 1.88's linear Needleman-Wunsch corner prints 12 499), **125 MB allocated against 835 MB of full matrices**. Targeted tier 329 / 0 (+ MCP Alignment 230 / 0). **Commit.** 97ab02b4.

### F164 — ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / GENOMIC-COMMON-001 / ALIGN-STATS-001 / MOTIF-CONS-001 (audit A4-9, WP40): edge-case coverage of the new EMBOSS ports; one FALSE doc claim, two behaviour fixes
- **Defect.** (1) `DotMatcher` doc / code: "empty when a sequence is not longer than the window, where the program reads beyond the sequence" was **false**: a window equal to a sequence is a normal run (a = 12 nt, b = 10 nt, w = 10, threshold 5: one segment 1,1,10,10) and a longer window gives segments (w = 11: 1,1,11,11) because the program scores the zero terminator beyond the end as 0 (model equal to the binary on 300 / 300 rows with an overhang 0 - 9; with 10 - 80 the program reads uninitialised heap and crashed on 56 of 240 rows: the reproducible limit is the allocator's chunk size, 8 residues always); (2) dotmatcher with a nucleic first and a non-nucleic second sequence is "Error: Sequence is not nucleic" (ACD: `bsequence` follows `asequence`) - the port scored it; (3) showalign `-sbegin` / `-send` had no port; (4) consambig has no range option (measured: ignored); lower case, ambiguity codes, `*`, gap characters and the default matrix of dotmatcher, lower-case-only input / N runs longer than the word / short, identical, nested sequences of megamerger were never in an oracle.
- **Source.** `emboss/dotmatcher.c` (loops, reads past the string), `dotmatcher.acd`, `emboss/showalign.c` (`begin = ajSeqsetGetBegin - 1`), `ajax/core/ajseq.c` (`ajSeqsetGetBegin / End`), installed 6.6.0 binaries.
- **Fix.** `DotMatcher.Compute` (no early return, zeros beyond the end, `ArgumentException` for the nucleic / non-nucleic pair, docs); `ShowAlignOptions.Begin` / `End` (MCP `showalign` `sbegin` / `send`; positive 1-based, negative from the end, clamped, an end before the begin shows one column; the consensus is that of the whole alignment, numbers and ticks keep the whole-alignment positions); consambig: documented, no option needed.
- **Oracle numbers.** `generate_dotmatcher_edge_oracle.py` 720 rows (120 each: lower case, ambiguity codes, `*`, gap characters, default matrix incl. the type refusal 37 rows, window >= length) equal; `generate_megamerger_edge_oracle.py` 480 rows (lowerall / lowerone / nrun / short incl. status nomerge and abort) equal, no code change; `generate_showalign_range_oracle.py` 400 rows equal; `generate_consambig_range_oracle.py` 300 rows equal to the plain call. Targeted tier 45 / 0, MCP Analysis 474 / 0, MCP Alignment 230 / 0. **Commit.** de5a8969.

### F165 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A4-7, WP40): minimap2 frequent minimizers and the seeding options
- **Defect.** `CollectAnchors` threw `NotSupportedException` for a query minimizer with more than `mid_occ` occurrences; not provided: `-H`, the hidden `-T` (sdust), `-X`, `-f` / `-U` / `-e` / `--q-occ-frac`, `--for-only` / `--rev-only`.
- **Source.** minimap2 v2.26 `sketch.c`, `seed.c`, `map.c`, `sdust.c`, `index.c`, `options.c`, `main.c` (raw.githubusercontent.com, User-Agent `Seqeron-review/1.0`); installed minimap2 2.26-r1175.
- **Fix.** `MinimapSeedOptions`, `CollectSeeds` (`mid_occ` = max(min, quantile + 1) capped by max; **`-f x` with x >= 1 is the limit itself, `-f x,y` sets `max_occ`: main.c**; `mm_seed_select` with the ksort max-heap; `mm_seed_mz_flt`; repeat length; `-H` tiny queue; `sdust_core` / `mm_dust_minier`; `skip_seed` for `-X` / strand flags; the tandem / self flag bits of the anchors); `CollectAnchors` delegates. Anchor-count guard (A4-2) unchanged for what survives the filter.
- **Oracle numbers.** `minimap2_seed_oracle.jsonl.gz`: **1 980 cases / 275 673 anchors** (330 each: default frequent minimizers (94 with `rl > 0`), `-f` / `-U` / `-e` / `--q-occ-frac` (238 with `rl > 0`), `-H`, `-T`, `-X`, `--for-only` / `--rev-only`): anchors, `RS` and `mid_occ` equal on all. **Commit.** 8b5e16d1.

### F166 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A4-7, WP40): minimap2 mapping stage: primary / secondary hits, long join, RMQ, dv, mapq; MCP options
- **Defect.** No `mm_gen_regs` / `mm_set_parent` / `mm_select_sub`, no `-r b,B` long-join re-chaining (`mg_lchain_rmq`; the old oracle used `-r b,b` to keep it off), no `--rmq`, no `dv` / mapq / `rl`; `-p`, `-N`, `--mask-level` / `--mask-len` / `--hard-mask-level`, `--chain-gap-scale` / `--chain-skip-scale` / `--max-chain-skip` / `--max-chain-iter` not on the MCP tool.
- **Fix.** `MinimapMapOptions`, `Minimap2Chaining.Map` (= `mm_map_frag` without `-c`): `Minimap2Chaining.Rmq.cs` ports `krmq.h` (the AVL tree with range-minimum queries: insert, erase, rotations, rmq, interval, iterator, node by node - the minimum among equal priorities depends on the tree shape) and `mg_lchain_rmq`; `Hits.cs` ports `mm_gen_regs` (X31 / Wang hash of the query name, `hash64` of the first anchor), `mm_set_parent`, `mm_select_sub` (including the in-place `r[p]` read after earlier hits moved), `mm_sync_regs`, `mm_est_err`, `mm_filter_strand_retained`, `mm_set_mapq` (single precision). `-N 0` = `-N 5 --secondary=no` (`OutputSecondary`); `-s` is used only by the base-level alignment (no effect on chaining). MCP `minimap2_chains`: 26 new parameters and `mode = chains | paf`, result `hits`, `repeatLength`, `midOcc`.
- **Oracle numbers.** `minimap2_map_oracle.jsonl.gz`: **1 320 PAF cases** (330 each: `sec` 792 hits / 425 secondary, `lj` 649 hits, `rmq` 678 hits, `misc` 597 hits): columns 3 - 12 and `tp cm s1 s2 dv rl` of every line equal on all. Targeted tier 75 / 0 (+ MCP Alignment 232 / 0). Base-level alignment (`-c`) stays A3-9c2. **Commit.** 8b5e16d1.
