### F167 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A5-1, WP41): minimap2 `X31Hash` sign extension of the first byte and the C-string end of the query name
- **Defect.** `Minimap2Chaining.X31Hash` (`khash.h` `__ac_X31_hash_string`: `h = (khint_t)*s; for (++s; *s; ++s) h = (h << 5) - h + (khint_t)*s` over a SIGNED `char`) zero-extended the first byte (`uint h = bytes[0]`) while the loop sign-extended the rest, and it hashed the whole string although a C string ends at the first NUL. The hash of the query name orders chains of equal score (`mm_gen_regs`: `hash ^= ...; hash64(...)`), so a name starting with a byte >= 0x80 (any non-ASCII first character, UTF-8 multi-byte) or containing U+0000 gave another primary / secondary choice than the program. Repro (auditor probe, 60 random references with three equal copies of a 300 - 600 base segment, query = the segment): names starting with U+00E9 differed from `minimap2 2.26` on 57 / 60, with U+4E2D on 53 / 60 (port primary `0:802-1195`, program primary `0:202-595`); ASCII names 0 / 60.
- **Fix.** `uint h = (uint)(sbyte)bytes[0]` and the hash stops at the first NUL byte (`Minimap2Chaining.Hits.cs`).
- **Oracle numbers.** `generate_minimap2_map_oracle.py` got a fifth group `qname` (330 cases, own seed `QNAME_SEED`, so the first 1 320 rows are reproduced byte for byte: the regenerated JSONL of the four old groups is identical to the committed content): query names from a UTF-8 pool (names starting with a 2-, 3- or 4-byte character 189, a non-ASCII character later in the name, names with U+0000 99, ASCII), references with 2 - 4 identical copies of the query (forward / reverse), `-N` / `-p` on some; PAF columns 3 - 12 and `tp cm s1 s2 dv rl` of every line equal on 330 / 330 with the fix (`minimap2_map_oracle.jsonl.gz`: 1 650 cases now). **Tests.** `Minimap2Map_Tests.Map_NonAsciiAndNulQueryNames_EqualMinimap2` (new group + counters of the classes). **Commit.** c4f2e8229.

### F168 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A5-2, WP41): resource guards and cancellation polls of the T-Coffee pair dynamic programs
- **Defect.** `biphasic_pair` / `fast_pair` at 20 000 x 20 000 residues: bare `OutOfMemoryException` after 1.4 s / 3.0 s; `-dp_mode gotoh_pair_wise` allocated 1.6 GB (traceback `int[l1+1][l2+1]`) without a check; `viterbi_pair_wise` computed `nmt * (I + 1) * (J + 1)` in 32-bit `int` (overflow beyond about 20 700 x 20 700) and 400 multiplications per cell with no poll (a 25 000 x 25 000 call ran 100 s); `TCoffeeLalignTopAlignments(topAlignments = int.MaxValue)` threw `OutOfMemoryException` (`new Vertex[K]`); `biphasic_pair` / the FASTA band / `glocal` / `gotoh_pair_wise_lgp` / `_sw` / the FASTA diagonal scan had no cancellation poll (a token cancelled after 1 s was observed after 3.6 s / 1.6 s); `KmerAnalyzer.CreateFracMinHashSketch(k = int.MaxValue)` allocated `new byte[k]`.
- **Source / measurement.** Installed `t_coffee` 13.41 under a 4 GB address-space limit: see the Evidence file (the program has no limit of its own: "FAILED TO ALLOCATE REQUIRED MEMORY (vmalloc)", exit status 1; a segmentation fault for gotoh_pair_wise at 40 000 x 40 000).
- **Fix.** `SequenceAligner.TCoffeeLimits.cs`: public `TCoffeePairDpMaxBytes` = 2 GiB and `RequireTcPairDp` (the `ResourceLimit` style, 64-bit, before the allocation) in `biphasic_pair_wise` (48 bytes per cell), `glocal_pair_wise` (40), `gotoh_pair_wise_lgp` (32), `gotoh_pair_wise` / `_sw` (4), `viterbi_pair_wise` (44, `TcRequireViterbiFits`), the FASTA band (20 per row x flagged diagonal) and the diagonal arrays of `evaluate_diagonals_with_ktup` / `_with_clist` / `very_fast_pair_wise`; a poll per row in `TcBiphasicMasks`, `TcGlocalMasks`, `TcGotohLgpMasks`, `TcGotohSwMasks`, `TcViterbiMasks` (match probabilities and recursion), `TcMakeFastaGotoh`, `TcIdscorePair`, per k-tuple occurrence in `TcDiagonalsByKtup` and per residue in `TcDiagonalsByLibrary`; the vertex list of `TcSim` grows lazily (limit `TopNSimMaxVertices`, as the matcher port); `KmerAnalyzer.CreateFracMinHashSketch`: no scratch buffer when the k-mer table is empty (B09 for B06). `AlignmentCancellation.OnPoll`: the internal test seam used by `PollCancellation` (A5-4).
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_TCoffeeLimits_Tests` (11: every limit + exception text + allocation < 64 MB + the int-overflow lengths, lazily grown vertex list, empty sketch, pre-cancelled + cancelled at the middle poll inside each method). **Commit.** 4ec4e8bab.

### F169 — ALIGN-MULTI-001 / ALIGN-STATS-001 (audit A5-3, WP41): null / out-of-range contract of the secondary public entry points
- **Defect.** A reflection fuzzer over every public static method of the owned classes (null / empty / extreme arguments, one parameter varied at a time over ordinary baselines; harness in the session scratchpad, 779 methods of `Seqeron.Genomics.Alignment` / `Analysis` and the MCP `AlignmentTools` / `AnalysisTools`) found `NullReferenceException` / `IndexOutOfRangeException` / `KeyNotFoundException` / `OverflowException` where an `ArgumentException` family exception is the library's contract: (1) `MuscleGuideTree` / `MuscleLocalAlign` / `MuscleProfileAlign` / `MuscleProfileScore` / `MuscleRefine` / `MuscleRefineWindows` / `MuscleScoreAlignment` (+ `MuscleRun`) with a null list or row, and a character above U+00FF (`_isResidue[256]`); (2) `KalignProgram.Parse` / `Run`, `PoaProgram.Run`, `ProbConsProgram.Parse` / `Run` (and the MCP `kalign_command`, `poa_command`, `probcons_command`) with a null argument or a null file text; (3) `Minimap2Chaining.Map` with a null target; (4) `TCoffeeKtupSimilarity`, `TCoffeePrimaryLibraryEntries` with a null list / element; (5) `TCoffeeSlowPairAlign`, `TCoffeeBiphasicPairAlign`, `TCoffeeLalignTopAlignments` with a character that is not a letter (`IndexOutOfRangeException` in `TcMatrix.Score`); (6) `Ksw2Aligner.SimpleMatrix(m <= 0 / int.MinValue / huge)`, `ExtendZ` / `ExtendD` with `m = int.MaxValue` (`m * m` overflow); (7) `AlignmentFormats.Format(.., A2m)` without the `state` annotation (`KeyNotFoundException`); (8) `PoaRun` / MCP `multiple_align_poa` with a null name / residues and a matrix with more than 128 symbols or a symbol above 127 (`IndexOutOfRangeException` in the `int[128, 128]` score table).
- **Source / measurement (installed programs).** `t_coffee` 13.41: a `*`, `-` or blank in a FASTA sequence is dropped by the program's reader (`slow_pair`, `biphasic_pair`, `lalign_id_pair` give the rows of the cleaned sequence), `U+00E9` ends the program with "ERROR: COREDUMP" (exit status 1); `muscle3` drops a non-letter byte of an unaligned input; argv entries of a real program are never null.
- **Fix.** `SequenceAligner.RequireNoNullElements` / `RequireProgramCall` (the shared message style `ArgumentNullException(paramName, "<Row|Argument|Target|Sequence> i is null.")`) at every entry point above; `RequireTcResidues` (letters only, `ArgumentException` naming the index and code point) at the three T-Coffee stages; `Ksw2Aligner.SimpleMatrix` refuses `m` outside 1 .. 256 and scores that do not fit `int8_t`, `CheckInputs` multiplies in 64 bits; `FormatA2m`: `ArgumentException` with the Biopython `KeyError` note; `PoaRun` null name / residues, `PoaScoreMatrix.Parse`: more than 128 symbols or a symbol above 127 -> `ArgumentException`; MUSCLE `PrepareMsa`: a character above U+00FF -> `ArgumentException` (Row i, column j). The documented program-abort of `GenomicAnalyzer.FindTandemRepeatsEtandem(threshold = int.MinValue)` (`InvalidOperationException`: the program's SIGFPE) stays by design.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_NullContract_Tests` (10 test methods, 13 cases: one assertion per entry point with the parameter name, the program's own handling untouched), MCP `NullContractTests` (3). **Cross-batch escapes of the same fuzz run (information, other batches' files):** `SequenceAssembler` (B10: `FindAllOverlaps`, `FindOverlap`, `CalculateIdentity`, `CalculateStats`, `ComputeConsensus`, `ErrorCorrectReads`, `CalculateCoverage`, `AssembleOLC` NullReference), `ApproximateMatcher` (B04: `FindWithMismatches` / `FindWithEdits` NullReference, `FindFrequentKmersWithMismatches*` OutOfMemory), `RnaSecondaryStructure` (B11 owner: `CalculateStemEnergy` / `CalculateHairpinLoopEnergy` NullReference, IndexOutOfRange + hangs in the MFE / pseudoknot / partition-function entry points), `SequenceComplexity` (B04: `MaskLowComplexity`, `FindLowComplexityIntervals` OutOfMemory, `CalculateWindowedComplexity` IndexOutOfRange), `ComparativeGenomics.CalculateReversalDistance` (B18 NullReference), `KmerAnalyzer` (B06: `GenerateAllKmers` OutOfMemory / hang, `SelectMarkovOrder` / `MarkovOrderBics` Overflow / OutOfMemory), `ProteinMotifFinder` (`PredictCoiledCoils` Overflow, `FindDomainEnvelopes` hang), `RepeatFinder` (`FindApproximateTandemRepeats`, `MaskApproximateTandemRepeats` hang). **Commit.** 02e96c3df.

### F170 — MCP tool documents (audit A5-5, WP41): generated from the code's tool metadata, checked by a contract test
- **Defect.** 80 of the 216 documents of the Alignment server and the B09 tools of the Analysis server were stale against the `[McpServerTool]` / `[Description]` attributes (A5-5 counted 21 with a description difference at first look: the strict parameter comparison also finds 59 documents with missing defaults, parameter descriptions or Input Schema rows): missing parameters `local_align_top_n tieOrder`, `sum_of_pairs_score matrix`, `tcoffee_primary_library genomicTemplates`, `find_open_reading_frames` (`strand`, `ignoreNested`, `regionStart`, `regionEnd`, `orfFinderMinLength`, `allowDualCodingStopTables`), `find_pattern_hits sequenceAmbiguity`, `multiple_align_poa case`; stale descriptions of `format_alignment`, `merger`, `stretcher_align`, `write_alignment`, `fuzztran`, `generate_emboss_consensus`, ...; missing `default` of the needle / water / supermatcher / needleall penalties.
- **Fix.** `scripts/docs/McpToolDump` (a reflection dumper of every `[McpServerTool]`: name, description, parameters with type, default and `[Description]`) + `scripts/docs/sync_mcp_tool_docs.py` (regenerates `description`, `inputSchema.properties` (names, order, type, `default`, description, `required`) of the `.mcp.json` and the Input Schema table (and the description paragraphs when they are the old tool description) of the `.md`; hand text of the documents - output schema, examples, errors, hand-written summaries - is kept; `--check` lists every difference and exits 1). All B09 documents regenerated; `gen-catalog.py` regenerated + `--check` fresh (548 tools), link check OK. The anti-drift guard is a contract test over reflection, shared by the MCP test projects: `ToolDocsContractTestsBase` (`Seqeron.Mcp.Tests.Contracts`) with the fixtures `Seqeron.Mcp.Alignment.Tests.ToolDocsContractTests` (all 76 tools) and `Seqeron.Mcp.Analysis.Tests.ToolDocsContractTests` (the tools whose `methodId` names a B09 class): it fails when a tool's description, parameter set / order, type, default, parameter description, `required` or the `.md` table differ from its document. **Commit.** 23837b449.

### F171 — test quality of the WP39 / WP40 tests (audit A5-4, WP41): deterministic cancellation, no wall-clock latency assertions, exact values
- **Defect.** `CancelledWhileRunning_ShowAlign_StopsPromptly` failed under CPU load (the timed cancel landed in an earlier stage) and the 13 mid-run tests of A4-1 / A3-5 used the same wall-clock pattern; about 20 `Elapsed < N s` assertions ran on shared machines; `Is.Not.Empty` / `Is.GreaterThan(0)` assertions where the exact result of the program is known.
- **Fix.** `AlignmentCancellation.OnPoll` (internal, `AsyncLocal`: it flows to `Parallel.For` workers and does not touch other tests) + the test helper `PollCancellation.CancelInside(work, "Method", nth)`: the call runs ONCE and the token is cancelled at the n-th poll whose stack names the method (no sleep, no thread, no time bound; "polled inside X" and "mid-run" are asserted; the inputs shrank, e.g. 3 M-base suffix trees -> 300 kb); `SequenceAligner_Cancellation_Tests` (every `CancelledWhileRunning_*` test names its poll site); the direct-token polls of the B09 `GenomicAnalyzer*.cs` scans now call `AlignmentCancellation.Poll(token)` (the same check, plus the ambient scope and the seam). Wall-clock assertions: removed where an allocation bound already proves the point (`SequenceAligner_ResourceLimits_Tests` x2), replaced by deterministic allocation proxies (`GlobalCoOptimal` cap, `ShowAlign` width, `FindPalindromes`), `TandemRepeat` 2 kb baseline asserts the exact unit, the 1 Mbp complexity guards of the oracle tests keep a bound that is 10x the earlier one with the justification in a comment. Exact values: `Minimap2Seeds_Tests` / MCP `Minimap2ChainsTests` poly-A case = `mid_occ = 10`, `RS 24`, 35 anchors of `minimap2 --print-seeds`, `Stretcher_*` score 20 (installed `stretcher`), `CollectAnchors` of the ordinary pair = 1 866 anchors (installed minimap2). **Commit.** b04254759.

### F172 - EMBOSS programs (audit A5-7, WP42): the sequence-input qualifiers `-sbegin -send -sreverse -slower -supper -scircular -snucleotide -sprotein` of the 16 programs
- **Defect.** None of the ported programs accepted the qualifiers every EMBOSS program has for each input; the results of ranged / reverse-complemented input (positions, strand, numbering direction, offsets) were not available. Defects found on the way: `WaterAlign` returned the EMPTY alignment for a one-residue sequence (installed water 6.6.0 reports the best single column with the score -FLT_MAX: `ACGT` / `G` -> `G` at 3); fuzztran printed no coordinates for the reverse frames ("meaningless numbers", B09 F140 note) although they are deterministic (`S - |frame| - 3 start + 4`, with `S` = the range END for frame -3 of `-frame R` / `6`).
- **Source.** EMBOSS 6.6 `ajacd.c` (`acdSetSeq` / `acdSetSeqall` / `acdSetSeqset`), `ajseq.c` (`ajSeqSetRange`, `ajSeqReverseDo`, `ajSeqTrim`, `ajSeqIsNuc`, `ajSeqsetReverse`), `ajseqread.c`, `ajseqtype.c` (`ajSeqTypeCheckIn`, `seqTypeFix`), `ajmath.c` (`ajCvtSposToPosStart`), `ajalign.c`, the program sources of the 16 programs and `fuzztran.c` (raw.githubusercontent.com/kimrutherford/EMBOSS, `Seqeron-review/1.0`); installed EMBOSS 6.6.0.
- **Fix.** `EmbossSequenceQualifiers` (Infrastructure; `Parse` of a command-line fragment) + `EmbossInputSequence.Read` (Alignment: type check / repair, case, reverse complement with the `ajBaseComp` table, mirrored range, `ajSeqTrim`) -> `EmbossSequenceView`; `EmbossSequence.Qualifiers`, `EmbossProgramResult.MatrixName`, `EmbossAlignedSequence.WithView` (the two offsets swap sides for a reversed sequence); `NeedleProgram` / `WaterProgram` / `StretcherProgram` / `MatcherProgram` / `MergerProgram` (merger does not trim: whole sequences merged, `-sbegin` / `-send` have no effect; measured 180 / 180) / `MegaMerge(EmbossSequence, EmbossSequence, ..)` (megamerger merges the trimmed texts), qualifier-aware `Supermatcher` / `NeedleAll` (stream / set quirks measured: later `seqall` members keep the first member's offsets when only an end is given, a `seqset` decides the reversal from its first member), `FindPatternHits` / `FindProteinPatternHits` / `FindTranslatedPatternHits` / `FindTandemRepeatsEtandem` / `FindTandemRepeatsEquicktandem` / `DotMatcher.Compute` / `SeqMatchAll` (`SeqMatchAllHit`) / `CalculateDistanceMatrix` overloads with the qualifiers (per-program behaviour in `docs/algorithms/Alignment/Pairwise_Output_Formats_And_EMBOSS_Programs.md` section 9: etandem / seqmatchall / distmat ignore the range, dotmatcher scans the whole length over the cut text, fuzztran cuts the reversed text at the begin / end as given); `WaterAlign` aligns a one-residue sequence like the program; `TranslatedPatternHit.EmbossStart` / `EmbossEnd` of the reverse frames. MCP parameter `sequenceQualifiers` on 12 tools; `MultipleAlignmentDisplay.ShowAlign` calls `EmbossInputSequence.PositionStart` (the local `SposToPosStart` copy is removed: dedup of A5-7).
- **Oracle numbers.** `emboss_seqopts_oracle.tsv` (`generate_emboss_seqopts_oracle.py`, seed 20261127): 16 programs x 180 rows (needle, water, stretcher, matcher, supermatcher, needleall, merger, megamerger, seqmatchall, fuzznuc, fuzzpro, fuzztran, etandem, equicktandem, dotmatcher, distmat), 2 880 rows with random ranges (inside / at / beyond the ends / negative / zero / reversed), reverse, case, circular, type overrides and the refused combinations (3 - 17 % per program): all equal, including the whole pair-format text of the six aligners.
- **Tests.** `EmbossSequenceQualifiers_Oracle_Tests` (16 oracle tests + reader / parser / reverse-complement table unit tests), MCP `EmbossSequenceQualifiersToolsTests` (Alignment, Analysis; `merger` / `megamerger` `sequenceQualifiers` included). **Commit.** a54b4c74.

### F173 - ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A5-9, WP42): minimap2 `-x` presets, `-F`, `-G`, the spliced chaining and the PAF text
- **Defect.** No preset factory (`mm_set_opt`), no `-F` / `-G`, no `is_cdna` chaining (spliced queries), no short-read mode, no PAF text writer.
- **Source.** minimap2 v2.26 `options.c`, `main.c`, `map.c`, `lchain.c`, `format.c`, `hit.c` (raw.githubusercontent.com, `Seqeron-review/1.0`); installed minimap2 2.26-r1175.
- **Fix.** `MinimapPresets.Apply` (all 11 names of the 2.26 table; the base-level members in `MinimapMapOptions.Scoring`), `MinimapPresets.WithMaxIntronLength` (`-G`, splice mode only), `MinimapMapOptions.MaxGapRef` / `MaxFragmentLength` / `ShortRead` / `Splice`, `MinimapChainOptions.IsCdna` (`comput_sc`: `min(linear, log)` penalty for a deletion, `max_dist_y` not raised, `max_drop` = infinity), no `mm_est_err` / `dv` for short reads, `Minimap2Chaining.FormatPaf`; MCP `minimap2_chains` (`preset`, `maxFragmentLength`, `maxIntronLength`, result `paf`).
- **Oracle numbers.** `minimap2_preset_oracle.jsonl.gz` (`generate_minimap2_preset_oracle.py`, seed 20261210): 11 presets x 220 random cases (also `-F` with `sr`, `-G` with every preset), the whole stdout of `minimap2 -x PRESET ref.fa query.fa` compared as TEXT: **2 420 / 2 420 equal**. Carried: the multi-segment fragment mode of `-x sr` with paired reads (A5-9b), the base-level `-c` stage (A3-9c2).
- **Tests.** `Minimap2Presets_Tests` (11 oracle tests, the options.c tables, `-G` only in splice mode, `FormatPaf`), MCP `Minimap2PresetToolsTests`. **Commit.** a54b4c74.

### F174 - ALIGN-MULTI-001 / GENOMIC-SIMILARITY-001 (audit A5-10, WP42): Mash `bounds`, `dist` filters / table, `triangle`
- **Defect.** Only `mash sketch` / `dist` numbers were ported; the text commands, the `-t` table, the `-v` / `-d` filters, `-i` and `triangle` were missing.

- **Fix.** `MashCommands.Bounds` / `Dist` / `Triangle` (new file; `StatisticsHelper.BinomialCdf`, bisection instead of the linear scan), MCP tools `mash_bounds`, `mash_dist`, `mash_triangle` (docs, catalog 551 tools, README, MCP_STATUS, traceability, registration test), `docs/algorithms/Alignment/Mash_Commands.md`.
- **Oracle numbers.** `mash_commands_oracle.jsonl.gz` (`generate_mash_commands_oracle.py`, seed 20261211): bounds 220 random (k, p), dist 220 (1 - 4 references x 1 - 3 queries, k 4 - 32, s 5 - 2000, seeds, -n, -i, -t, -v, -d), triangle 220 (-E, -v, -d, -i): **660 / 660 byte-equal**. Not ported (A5-10b): `mash screen`, the reads mode (`-r -m -b -c -g`), the alphabets (`-a -z -Z`), `taxscreen` / `paste` / `info`.
- **Tests.** `MashCommands_Oracle_Tests`, MCP `MashToolsTests`. **Commit.** a54b4c74.

### F175 - Cross-batch fuzz escapes (audit A5-3 follow-up, WP42 step 0): B04 / B05 / B06 guards, B10 / B12 / B14 / B18 registered
- **Defect / verification.** The fuzz findings of F169 for other batches' files were re-verified with a reflection probe (one parameter varied over null / empty / int.MinValue / -1 / 0 / int.MaxValue / NaN, 4 s hang limit; 6 classes x all public static methods): confirmed in `SequenceAssembler` (B10), `RnaSecondaryStructure` (B12), `ComparativeGenomics.CalculateReversalDistance` (B18), `ProteinMotifFinder.PredictCoiledCoils` (B14) -> registered in QUEUE.md "Known cross-batch items" with file:line, repro and planned guard; confirmed AND fixed in the finished batches' files: B05 `ApproximateMatcher` (null `DnaSequence` x3, `k > length` scratch buffer), B04 `SequenceComplexity` (`stepSize = int.MaxValue` overflow, `windowSize = int.MaxValue` SDUST window), B06 `KmerAnalyzer` (`GenerateAllKmers(k > string.MaxLength)`, `MarkovOrderBics` / `SelectMarkovOrder(maxOrder >= length)`); NOT reproduced (not recorded): the `RepeatFinder.FindApproximateTandemRepeats` "hang".
- **Tests.** `CrossBatchContract_B09_Tests`. **Commit.** 71da5d4e.

### F176 - EMBOSS programs (audit A5-8, WP42): the feature-report texts `-rformat gff` / `excel` of fuzznuc, fuzzpro, fuzztran, etandem, equicktandem
- **Defect.** The ports returned structured hits only; no report text.
- **Source.** EMBOSS 6.6 `ajreport.c` / `ajfeat.c` behaviour measured on the installed programs (`fuzznuc.c`, `fuzzpro.c`, `fuzztran.c`, `etandem.c`, `equicktandem.c` opened).
- **Fix.** One canonical layer: `EmbossReportWriter` (Infrastructure: `EmbossReport`, `EmbossReportFeature`, `Write(report, format, date)`) + `EmbossReports.Fuzznuc / Fuzzpro / Fuzztran / Etandem / Equicktandem` (Analysis); facts measured and implemented: nothing written for no hit (fuzz*) but the header (tandem), the `##sequence-region` end (sequence length / unsigned maximum of the coordinates), percent-encoded attribute values, zero score `.`, start-descending order of range patterns (end-descending on the complement strand; position order with mismatches), fuzznuc score = length - mismatches, fuzztran gff coordinate order rule. MCP `reportFormat` / `sequenceName` of `find_pattern_hits`, `fuzzpro`, `fuzztran`, `find_tandem_repeats_emboss`.
- **Oracle numbers.** `emboss_report_oracle.tsv` (`generate_emboss_report_oracle.py`, seed 20261212): 120 runs per program and format = **1 200 / 1 200 whole reports equal** (`#!Date` masked). Not written (A5-8b): seqtable / table / simple / motif / dbmotif / nametable / regions / listfile / tagseq / diffseq / draw / feattable / fastap / fasta / embl / genbank / srs / swiss / pir, `-raccshow` / `-rdesshow` / `-rscoreshow` / `-rstrandshow` / `-rusashow` / `-rmaxall` / `-rmaxseq`, the `distmat` matrix file.
- **Tests.** `EmbossReport_Oracle_Tests` (10 oracle tests + the audit example), MCP `ReportFormat_ReturnsTheProgramsReportText`. **Commit.** a54b4c74.

### F177 - EMBOSS programs (audit A6-2, WP43): the sequence-qualifier text is resolved as the ACD library of the program resolves it
- **Defect.** `EmbossSequenceQualifiers.Parse` accepted a strict subset of the command-line grammar and ignored the rest silently: (a) `-sbegin1 5` of a ONE-sequence program (`fuzznuc a.fa -pattern ACG -sbegin1 5` = `-sbegin 5`) was dropped (`Parse("-sbegin1 5", "").IsDefault` was true); (b) the unnumbered form after the files (`needle a.fa b.fa -sbegin 3` is `-sbegin2 3`: the qualifiers belong to the parameter that precedes them) was ignored for BOTH inputs; (c) `-sbegin1=5`, the unique abbreviations (`-sbeg1`, `-sre2`), `--sbegin`, `/sbegin`, `sbegin=5` and the boolean values (`-sreverse2 N`, `-slower1=yes`) were refused or dropped. **Correction of the audit claim:** the auditor's rule for the unnumbered form was incomplete: an abbreviation WITHOUT number after the second file (`-sbe 3`) applies to EVERY input (the master route is only taken when the general search finds the same item), and a numbered qualifier of another input or any non-sequence qualifier ends the tie (a later unnumbered qualifier then applies to all inputs): all measured below.
- **Source.** EMBOSS 6.6 `ajacd.c` (`acdArgsParse`, `acdIsQual`, `acdQualParse`, `acdFindQual`, `acdFindQualDetail`, `acdFindQualAssoc`, `acdFindQualMaster`, `acdTestQualC`, `acdTestAssoc`, `acdValIsBool`, `acdArgsScan`), `ajstr.c` (`ajStrToInt`, `ajStrToBool`, `ajStrIsFloat`, `ajStrPrefixS`, `ajStrIsAlnum`) (raw.githubusercontent.com mirrors of EMBOSS-6.6.0, `Seqeron-review/1.0`); installed EMBOSS 6.6.0: the `Commandline` echo of every program header and `EMBOSS_ACDLOG=1` (the ACD processing log: `set qualifier -sbegin[2] (param 0) = 3`, `set next qualifier ...` = the program's own statement of what each token set).
- **Fix.** `AcdCommandLine` (Infrastructure; a line-by-line port of the resolver for the qualifiers of the sequence inputs) over `EmbossAcdProfiles.g.cs` (the qualifier universe of each of the 16 programs, read from `<program> -help -verbose` by `generate_emboss_acd_profiles.py`: an abbreviation is unique only among ALL qualifiers of the program, `-se` is ambiguous in fuzznuc (`send,sequence`) and not in needle); `EmbossSequenceQualifiers.ParseInputs(program, text)` returns one entry per sequence input (1 or 2) and is used by the 16 MCP tools (`Parse(text, suffix)` stays, over a program-less profile); integer values = `strtol` decimal saturated to int (a 64-bit overflow is refused), boolean values = `ajStrToBool`; `-sformat -sask -squick -sdbname -sid -iquery -ufo -fformat -fopenfile` accepted without effect (residue strings have no file), `-ioffset` must be 0, every other qualifier or a stray word is refused (the program would take a stray word as a further parameter); the MCP `sequenceQualifiers` descriptions carry `EmbossSequenceQualifiers.CommandLineRules`.
- **Oracle numbers.** `emboss_acd_args_oracle.tsv` (`generate_emboss_acd_args_oracle.py`, seed 20261220): 16 programs x 130 random texts = **2 080 rows** (numbered / unnumbered names, unique and ambiguous abbreviations, `-` / `--` / `/` / no dash, any case, `=value`, `-no` negations, boolean and integer values the program accepts or refuses; 852 accepted, 1 016 refused by the program, 212 accepted by the program but not sequence qualifiers): the resolution (`name[pnum]=value`) of every accepted text and the refusal of every other equal on **2 080 / 2 080**; `emboss_seqopts_text_oracle.tsv` (`generate_emboss_seqopts_text_oracle.py`): 16 x 40 = **640 rows**, the same program runners as the A5-7 oracle (needle, water, stretcher, matcher, supermatcher, needleall, merger, megamerger, seqmatchall, fuzznuc, fuzzpro, fuzztran, etandem, equicktandem, dotmatcher, distmat) with the text after the sequence arguments: what the program REPORTS equal on 640 / 640.
- **Tests.** `EmbossAcdCommandLine_Oracle_Tests` (16), `EmbossSequenceQualifiersText_Oracle_Tests` (16), `EmbossSequenceQualifiers_CommandLine_Tests` (9: the forms of the audit, `-sbegin_asequence`, values, refusals with the program's messages), MCP `EmbossSequenceQualifiersToolsTests` (Alignment, Analysis: `fuzznuc -sbegin1 5`, `needle -sbegin 3 -send 8` = `-sbegin2 3 -send2 8`, `-sbe 3` on both, ambiguity). **Commit.** f49cacce.

### F178 - ALIGN-LOCAL-001 (audit A6-3, WP43): matcher `-gapopen` below `-gapextend`, integer options
- **Defect.** `MatcherProgram` refused every `-gapopen` below `-gapextend` (72 of 139 random rows: `-gapopen 0 .. 3` with the default `-gapextend 4` is a legal run of matcher 6.6.0; the first gap residue then costs less than a further one: SIM's `q = gapopen - gapextend` is negative; the scalar scoring rejected the positive `GapOpen`), `gapOpen = 1e308` was an `OverflowException` ("Value was either too large or too small for a Decimal"), `1.5` was accepted although the ACD says "Invalid integer value". **Correction of the audit claim:** the program does not die on a negative value, it RESETS it with a warning (`Warning: integer value out of range -1 less than (reset to) 0`; `-alternatives 0` -> 1) and runs; a value near `int.MaxValue` is UB in the program (its 32-bit scores overflow: `-gapopen 2147483647` prints a nonsense alignment with score 2147483647): the port refuses it by the integer-range check.
- **Fix.** `PairScoring.Create(.., allowPositiveGapOpen)` (internal; used only for `TopNTieOrder.Sim` with affine gaps), `SequenceAligner.EmbossInteger` (the ACD integer rule: finite, integral, 64-bit range, saturation, reset to the minimum with the warning), `MatcherProgram` reads `-alternatives`, `-gapopen`, `-gapextend` in the order of `matcher.acd`; the warnings are returned in the new `EmbossProgramResult.StandardError` (the program's stderr; `ErrorText` stays the error file).
- **Oracle numbers.** `matcher_gap_oracle.tsv` (`generate_matcher_gap_oracle.py`, seed 20261222): 872 rows (745 accepted by the program, 297 of them with `-gapopen` below `-gapextend`, 0 / 1 / 100, 36 with a reset warning; 127 refused or aborting; the 70 rows whose value is not a plain number - `abc`, `1e2`, the empty string - cannot be passed to the numeric API and are not compared): **all other rows equal**, alignment lists (score, coordinates, rows), the warning lines and the refusals.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_MatcherGap_Tests` (3). **Commit.** 5b52e5f5.

### F179 - ALIGN-MULTI-001 / GENOMIC-TANDEM-001 (audit A6-4, WP43): null / degenerate arguments of the EMBOSS sets, Mash inputs and the report layer
- **Defect.** `SequenceAligner.NeedleAll` / `Supermatcher` with a null element of a set (`EmbossSetInput.Any` dereferenced `s.Qualifiers`), a member without `Name` / `Residues` (also `NeedleProgram` / `WaterProgram` / `StretcherProgram` / `MatcherProgram` / `MergerProgram` / `MegaMerge`) -> `NullReferenceException`; `SeqMatchAll` named the loop variable (`ArgumentNullException("s")`); `MashCommands.Dist` / `Triangle` with a null list element / `Records` / `Name` / `Sequence` -> `NullReferenceException` or "Value cannot be null (source)"; `EmbossReports.Fuzztran` with a hit made by hand (`default(TranslatedPatternHit)`): `InvalidOperationException` "Nullable object must have a value"; `EmbossReportWriter.Write` with `(EmbossReportFormat)9999` wrote GFF, with a null feature / field `NullReferenceException`.
- **Fix.** `SequenceAligner.RequireEmbossSet` / `RequireEmbossSequence` (`ArgumentNullException(paramName, "Sequence i is null." / "... has no Residues.")`), `RequireNoNullElements` in both `SeqMatchAll` overloads, `MashCommands.Sketches` (parameter names `references` / `queries` / `inputs`), `EmbossReports.*` (null names, `Fuzztran`: `ArgumentException` "Hit i has no EmbossStart / EmbossEnd"), `EmbossReportWriter.Write`: `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` for an undefined format and `ArgumentException` for a null field of the report or of a feature.
- **Tests.** `EmbossNullContract_Tests` (5), `MashCommands_Oracle_Tests` (null contract, parameter range). **Commit.** 12f13370 / 5b52e5f5.

### F180 - ALIGN-LOCAL-001 (audit A6-5, WP43): supermatcher `-wordlen` above the targets
- **Defect.** `Supermatcher(.., wordLength = int.MaxValue)` spent 30 s in the Rabin-Karp loops (`SupermatcherWords.Search` runs `wordLength - 1` multiplications and `wordLength` hash steps per query whatever the text length, no poll) and returned only the error-file text. **Measured on supermatcher 6.6.0:** the program loops too (`-wordlen 2147483647`: 8.9 s, a segmentation fault from about `-wordlen 1000000`, exit status 139) but prints `Error: no kmers found` on stderr whenever its word table is EMPTY: every `-wordlen` above the longest target (`-wordlen 24` / `30` with 23-base targets; not at `-wordlen 23`), or targets that hold only N / X runs; the error file lists every pair as usual; the exit status is 0.
- **Fix.** An empty word table returns before the loops: `EmbossProgramResult.StandardError` = `Error: no kmers found\n`, the error file as before (the loops cannot find anything; the program's crash for a huge word length is UB and not reproduced); the per-query poll stays. `SeqMatchAll` / `MegaMerge` / `DotMatcher` with `int.MaxValue` were verified (no hang: empty result / the program's `No match found` refusal).
- **Oracle numbers.** `supermatcher_wordlen_oracle.tsv` (`generate_supermatcher_wordlen_oracle.py`, seed 20261223): 260 rows (word length around, at and above the longest target and 100 / 1 000 / 100 000 / 999 999; 140 with `Error: no kmers found`): stderr, error file and the alignment list (names, scores) **260 / 260 equal**.
- **Tests.** `SequenceAligner_SupermatcherWordLength_Tests` (4: the oracle, `-wordlen int.MaxValue` returns at once, N-only targets, the sibling programs). **Commit.** 5b52e5f5.

### F181 - ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A6-7, WP43): minimap2 `-x PRESET` followed by an option whose value equals the default
- **Defect.** MCP `minimap2_chains` merged the preset with `Pick(given, default, fromPreset)` / `||` / `&&`: `k = 15`, `w = 10`, `minChainScore = 40`, `bestN = 5`, `rmq = false`, `outputSecondary = true` were indistinguishable from "not given" (`-x asm5 -k 15` is k = 15 in the program; the tool used the preset's 19), and a boolean could only be switched on. **Correction of the audit claim:** `-H` has NO negation in minimap2 2.26 (`main.c`: `ipt.flag |= MM_I_HPC`), so "`-H` off with `map-pb`" is not possible in the program either; the options with a value are `--rmq=yes|no`, `--secondary=yes|no` (and `--dual=yes|no`), the flags `-H -X -P --no-long-join --hard-mask-level --for-only --rev-only` only switch on.
- **Fix.** `MinimapOverrides` (nullable members = "not given") + `MinimapPresets.Apply(preset, overrides)` (the preset, then every given option, `-G` last; the ONE merge: it replaces the third copy of the field list in the MCP layer); `minimap2_chains` takes `int? k`, `int? w`, `int? maxGap`, `int? bandwidth`, `int? minChainScore`, `int? minAnchors`, `double? midOccFraction`, `int? minMidOcc` / `maxMidOcc`, `int? occDist`, `int? bandwidthLong`, `int? maxChainSkip`, `int? bestN`, `double? primaryRatio`, `bool? rmq`, `bool? outputSecondary`, `int? maxFragmentLength`, `int? maxIntronLength`, ... (null = not given; the flags stay `bool`), `mode = chains` uses the same resolved options.
- **Oracle numbers.** `minimap2_preset_oracle.jsonl.gz` got 40 `-x PRESET <options>` cases per preset after the A5-9 cases (the first 2 420 rows are byte-identical to the committed file; options `-k -w -g -r -n -m -p -N -e -f -U --max-chain-skip -M --rmq= --secondary= -H --no-long-join`, half of them with the DEFAULT value): 11 x 260 = **2 860 / 2 860 PAF texts equal** (the whole stdout).
- **Tests.** `Minimap2Presets_Tests` (exact counts per preset: cases, cases with `-F` / `-G`, cases with overrides, PAF lines), MCP `Minimap2PresetToolsTests` (whole PAF lines of `minimap2 -x asm5 -k 15`, `-w 10 -m 40 -n 3`, `-x ava-ont -m 40`). **Commit.** f78e1d38.

### F182 - GENOMIC-SIMILARITY-001 / ALIGN-MULTI-001 (audit A6-1 / A6-6, WP43; B09 for B06): streaming MinHash sketches, Mash refusals
- **Defect.** `KmerAnalyzer.CreateMinHashSketch` / `CreateFracMinHashSketch` built a `string` per distinct k-mer (`CountKmers` -> `HashSet<string>`) before hashing: auditor measurement 1 Mbp 1.8 s / 285 MB, 5 Mbp 9.3 s / 1.2 GB, 20 Mbp 40 s / 4.1 GB (`mash sketch` of the same genome: about 1 s, constant memory); `MashCommands` / `mash_dist` / `mash_triangle` inherited it and had no cancellation poll. **A6-6:** `mash dist -k 11 g.fa short.fa` (every record of a file shorter than k) exits with status 1 and "WARNING: All fasta records in input files were shorter than the k-mer size (11)." (or `ERROR: Did not find fasta records in "input files".` for a file without records: **correction of the audit claim:** the program prints the words "input files", not the file name); the port printed a result line. Two more crashes of the program have no output to mirror: `mash dist -i` with no reference sequence of k symbols (SIGFPE), `mash triangle -i` of sequences all shorter than k without `-E` (`std::out_of_range` abort) -> refused.
- **Fix.** A rolling 2-bit forward / reverse-complement window (k <= 32; two byte buffers above, FracMinHash has no k limit) hashed with the same MurmurHash3 input bytes, a bounded sorted set of the bottom-s hashes (Mash `MinHashHeap`) / the hashes <= max_hash (sourmash); optional trailing `CancellationToken` (a poll per 4 096 windows and per record); `MashCommands.Dist` / `Triangle` take a token (per input, per pair; the MCP tools pass theirs) and refuse a file without a record of k symbols with the program's text. Measured now (Release, the same genomes): 1 Mbp 0.11 s / 0.4 MB, 5 Mbp 0.26 s / 0.4 MB, 20 Mbp **0.83 s / 0.5 MB** (FracMinHash 1.05 s / 2 MB).
- **Oracle numbers.** The previous implementation is the test oracle (`KmerAnalyzer_SketchStreaming_Tests`): **1 200** random record sets for Mash (canonical and forward, k 1 .. 32, s 1 .. 100 000, case / N / IUPAC / U, null and short records, 708 non-empty) and **1 200** random sequences for FracMinHash (k up to 60, scaled 1 .. 1 000, abundance on / off, 535 non-empty, 100+ with k > 32), all 65 536 UTF-16 code units; the existing 440 + 660 Mash rows and the sourmash FracMinHash oracle unchanged; `mash_commands_oracle.jsonl.gz` got 80 `error` rows (`generate_mash_commands_oracle.py`, the program's non-zero statuses are kept: 1 and -8): status, message and exception equal **80 / 80**.
- **Tests.** `KmerAnalyzer_SketchStreaming_Tests` (8: equality, code units, allocation < 16 MB for 4 Mbp, cancellation), `MashCommands_Oracle_Tests` (error rows, null contract, cancellation), MCP `MashToolsTests`. **Commit.** 12f13370 (B06.md "Post-completion changes").

### F183 - tests, documents and the report mode (audit A6-8 / A6-9 / A6-10, WP43)
- **A6-8 (tolerant tests).** `Minimap2Presets_Tests` asserts the exact number of cases, `-F` / `-G` cases, override cases and PAF lines per preset (the counter `Is.GreaterThan(0).Or.EqualTo(0)` is gone); `GenomicAnalyzer_ProteinPatterns_Oracle_Tests`: `rejected` is exactly 0 (the fuzztran oracle holds 760 successful runs, no refused row); MCP wiring tests assert the program's WHOLE result: `Dotmatcher_Qualifiers_...` the 22 segments of `dotmatcher -sbegin1 10`, `Etandem_IgnoresTheRange_Equicktandem_UsesIt` the two repeats of `equicktandem ... -sbegin 10 -send 40` and the two of the whole run, `Seqmatchall_Reverse_NumbersFromTheEnd` the 42 matches of `seqmatchall -wordsize 3 -sreverse`, `Minimap2PresetToolsTests` whole PAF lines, `MashToolsTests` the whole text of `mash bounds -k 9 -p 0.9`; the `[Explicit]` fixture `SequenceAligner_MultipleAlign_Benchmark` (never run by a tier, ended in `Assert.Pass`) is replaced by `SequenceAligner_MultipleAlignAnchored_Scenarios_Tests` (8 deterministic scenarios: every input aligned, equal rows, gaps removed = input, for the exact center star and the anchored aligner) and the timing table moved to `apps/Seqeron.Alignment.Benchmarks`.
- **A6-9 (documents).** The XML docs of `MinimapMapOptions.Splice` and the `MinimapPresets` remarks no longer say that the `is_cdna` chaining is missing (A5-9: `ChainOptions` sets `IsCdna = Splice`, `splice` / `splice:hq` equal the program); unit reports, TestSpecs and Evidence of ALIGN-MULTI-001, ALIGN-STATS-001, ALIGN-GLOBAL-001, ALIGN-LOCAL-001, GENOMIC-COMMON-001, GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001, GENOMIC-TANDEM-001 and GENOMIC-SIMILARITY-001 carry the round-5 and round-6 entries (A5-1 ... A5-3, A5-7 ... A5-10, A6-1 ... A6-10); `Mash_Commands.md` is linked from `Sequence_Similarity.md` and `CANONICAL_MAP.md` (and links both).
- **A6-10 (report mode).** `find_tandem_repeats_emboss`, `fuzzpro` and `fuzztran` with `reportFormat` run the library search ONCE (the plain result and the report are built from the same hits; `find_pattern_hits` already did); one helper (`ReportFormatWithoutQualifiers`) for the format parse and the refusal together with `sequenceQualifiers` in the four tools. **Tests.** MCP `EmbossReportSingleSearchTests` (the scans poll the ambient seam once per window: the same number of polls with and without `reportFormat`).

## Duplication removed

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 / REPEAT (audit A2-14, F104): the exact `printf("%.Nf")` formatter (BigInteger, ties to even) was written twice - `RepeatFinder.FormatCFixed` (TRF output) and a new one for the ClustalW guide-tree files; both now call `CFormat.Fixed` (Infrastructure, additive); `RepeatFinder.FormatCFixed` stays as a one-line delegate for its tests. `SequenceAligner.CreateSets` of the ClustalW state (private, steps of a binary NJ tree) was replaced by the faithful `groupSeqs` port `ClustalWCreateSets`, which also serves user trees - one implementation for both.

- GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001: `GenomicAnalyzer.FindMotif` was a copy of `MotifFinder.FindExactMotif` (same suffix-tree search, differing in sorting/validation) → delegates (F18); `FindKnownMotifs` no longer carries its own suffix-tree loop for few motifs but calls the canonical KMP `KmerAnalyzer.FindKmerPositions`; IUPAC and reverse-complement handling call `MotifFinder.FindDegenerateMotif` / `DnaSequence.GetReverseComplementString` (no new matcher).

- ALIGN-GLOBAL-001: the cancellable overload `GlobalAlign(string,string,scoring,ct,progress)` re-implemented the full NW fill/traceback; all 4 overloads now share `GlobalAlignCore` (same diagonal>up>left tie order, same progress/cancellation points). Pooled-buffer copy path removed. Behaviour change: empty input to the typed cancellable overload now returns the NW all-gap alignment (consistent with the typed non-cancellable overload) instead of `AlignmentResult.Empty`.

- ALIGN-SEMI-001: `SemiGlobalAlignCore` duplicated the NW fill; removed — `GlobalAlignCore` and semi-global both use `LinearAlignCore(…, fitting)`. Byte-identical outputs on 1628 cases.

- ALIGN-MULTI-001: `ReconcileAlignments` + `MultipleAlignClassic` no longer carry two merge variants (one `MergeCenterAlignments`); the T-Coffee primary-library loop existed twice (`BuildExtendedLibrary` and `BuildPrimaryLibraryForDiagnostics`) → one `BuildPrimaryLibrary`; private 4-mer hash → Core `CountKmersSpan`; the progressive/iterative/consistency aligners share `PairwiseIdentityDistance` / `BuildProgressiveGuideTree` (UPGMA is re-implemented here only because Phylogenetics depends on Alignment, circular reference — B09 sweep to re-check).

- GENOMIC-ORF-001: the hard-coded `StopCodons` / `IsStopCodon` / `StartCodon` constants → `GeneticCode` (via `OrfScanner`); the private per-frame ORF loop of `GenomicAnalyzer` and the character loop of `Translator.FindOrfsInSequence` (B02, "(B09 for B02)") → one shared `Core/OrfScanner.FindRegions` (DUP_MAP §2 hard-coded stops, §3 ORF engines); reverse strand → canonical `DnaSequence.GetReverseComplementString`.

- GENOMIC-REPEAT-001: `GenomicAnalyzer.FindPalindromes` (verbatim copy of `RepeatFinder.FindPalindromesCore`, DUP_MAP §17) → delegates to `RepeatFinder.FindPalindromes` (F27); `FindLongestRepeat` now uses the canonical `SuffixTree.FindAllLongestRepeatedSubstrings` (F29) instead of the tree's single-representative bookkeeping; `FindRepeats` / `FindMaximalRepeats` share one traversal collector. `FindRepeats` does **not** call `RepeatFinder.FindDirectRepeats` / `SuffixTree.FindMaximalRepeatedPairs`: their result set is maximal pairs, different from "all repeated substrings" (documented, F28).

- GENOMIC-SIMILARITY-001: private `GetKmers` + intersection/union loop of `GenomicAnalyzer.CalculateSimilarity` -> `KmerAnalyzer.JaccardSimilarity * 100` (F32, B06 request DUP_MAP §16).

- GENOMIC-TANDEM-001: `GenomicAnalyzer.FindTandemRepeatsCore` (a stand-alone exact tandem scan, DUP_MAP §17 "4 engines") → facade over `RepeatFinder.FindMicrosatellites` (F35). The remaining exact tandem engine outside B04 is `GenomeAnnotator`'s private scan (B11, minimum span + unit lengths up to 60; `FindMicrosatellites(…, maxUnit: 60)` + a span filter would replace it — request registered for B11 below); `GenomeAssemblyAnalyzer.FindTandemRepeats` (B10, 80 % purity) is legitimately different. The new `Analysis/MaximalRepetitions.cs` is the only runs engine in the repository (it reuses `SequenceComplexity.BuildSuffixArray` / `BuildLcpArray`, no new suffix-array code).

- ALIGN-GLOBAL/LOCAL/SEMI (A1-2, F41): the substitution-matrix aligners add no dynamic program: `PairScoring` feeds the existing linear/local/affine cores and traceback (scalar path = scale 1), so tie order, coordinates and empty-input contracts are identical by construction. ALIGN-STATS (A1-9, F43): the `infoalign` consensus reference calls `MotifFinder.GenerateEmbossConsensus` (B05) instead of a second `cons`.

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (A1-12, F53): the private UPGMA (`BuildProgressiveGuideTree`, a copy of `PhylogeneticAnalyzer.BuildUPGMA`) is deleted — the guide tree is built by the canonical `PhylogeneticAnalyzer.BuildTreeFromMatrix` (UPGMA and NJ) through the additive `TreeBuildOptions`; the unused `Phylogenetics -> Alignment` project reference that "justified" the copy is removed. (A1-16, F54): `MultipleAlignClassic` (a second star-alignment path used by one explicit benchmark) removed from production code.

- ALIGN-LOCAL-001 / ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 (A2-28c, F124): the matrix / residue-code table that `NeedleCore` and `WaterCore` each built is one `EmbossScoreTable` (also used by supermatcher, needleall, `SimilaritySubHeader`). Duplication that stays: stretcher's row-wise score table (per-residue rows for its Myers-Miller scans); `Ksw2Aligner` next to `GlobalAlignDualAffine` (the SIMD recurrence is the program's, not the mathematical DP); the Biopython / EMBOSS / ClustalW writers of the same format names (F126: different bytes).

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (A2-23, F127): the ProbCons pair-HMM (forward / backward / log-sum / EXP / parameter tables) is T-Coffee's `proba_pair` engine, shared (`TcProbaModel` second constructor, `TcProbaBackward` `endMatch`); the Defaults.h tables were verified equal to `TcProbaTables` (no second copy). Duplication that stays: `PcSparse` / the sparse consistency transformation next to `TCoffeeLibrary`'s relaxation (different data structure, normalisation and float order), the three-way maximum-expected-accuracy DP next to the other global DPs (the program's tie order).

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (A2-26, F128): the POA graph DP is the program's own affine model with an explicit gap-length state (truncation / decay arrays, special initial-gap state); it is not a variant of `GlobalAlignAffine` and shares no code. `PoaScoreMatrix` is a third matrix-file reader (format differs from `SubstitutionMatrix` / `TcMatrix`).

- EMBOSS reader (audit A4-3, F153): the `ajSeqIsNuc` rule was written five times (`DotMatcher.IsNucleotide`, `MotifFinder.IsEmbossNucleotideSequence` + `EmbossNucGapChars`, `SequenceAligner.IsNucleotideText`, `PairWordMatcher`'s `IsNucleotideWordSequence`, `EmbossAlignmentWriter.IsNuc`) with three different alphabets (the megamerger / seqmatchall copy lacked X and the gap characters); all five now call `EmbossSequenceReader.IsNucleotide` (Infrastructure; the megamerger 600 / 600 and seqmatchall 330 / 330 oracles stay equal), and the cleaning of the input sequences exists once (`EmbossSequenceReader.Clean` / `CleanGapped`) instead of in none of the 20 entry points.

## Simplifications kept (with reason) / LIMITATIONS proposals

- ALIGN-STATS-001: none for the counted statistics. The markup reproduces EMBOSS for exactly two sequences (EMBOSS's >2-sequence consensus statistics are the `cons` program, implemented in `MotifFinder.GenerateEmbossConsensus` by B05). Residue-pair matrices other than BLOSUM62 / PAM250 / EDNAFULL are available through the public `SubstitutionMatrix` constructor, not as built-ins (the three are the ones EMBOSS needle/water use by default or the unit validates against).

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (declared in the algorithm doc §5.3): (1) linear-gap model for all MSA methods, `GapOpen` unused — the exact SP objective with affine natural gap costs (Altschul 1989) depends on the whole column history of every row pair; MSA programs use quasi-natural/position-specific approximations and no exact polynomial profile-profile DP exists; the 2-sequence case is covered by `GlobalAlignAffine`. (2) ~~UPGMA guide tree instead of NJ for the T-Coffee path~~ **resolved (A1-12, F53): `MsaOptions.GuideTree = NeighborJoining`, ClustalW's tree and order verified against clustalw 2.1**; (3) single best SW alignment per pair instead of Lalign's top-N non-intersecting alignments and no sequence-redundancy weights. (2) and (3) change merge order / library density only. (4) the program's progressive stage (relaxation + linked pair-wise DP) is available as `MsaConsistencyEngine.TCoffeeLinked` / `MultipleAlignTCoffee` (A1-13, F58), exact for an explicit library + tree; the program's own primary libraries, k-tuple NJ tree and default method are `MultipleAlignTCoffeeProgram` (A1-13b, F66).
- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (A1-10c a / a2, F65 / F69 / F70 / F76): `-maxiters 1 / 2` of `MultipleAlignMuscle` equals the MUSCLE 3.8.1551 binary on 420 / 420 user-run sets (F69); every other option and command line of the program (iterations >= 3, `-anchors`, `-objscore`, `-weight*`, `-distance*`, neighbour joining + `-root*`, `-brenner`, `-usetree`, `-tree1/-tree2/-distmx*/-computeweights`, `-profile`, `-refine`, `-spscore`, `-ppscore`, `-maketree`, `-refinew`, `-sw`) equals it on 630 / 630 recorded sets of the second user-run round (F76, after six defects were fixed). Residual: `-matrix` of a file without the `*` column (8 unrecorded sets) and of nucleotides, corner cases: round 3 package `handoff/A1-10c-muscle-r3` awaits the user's run (A1-10c a3).
- ALIGN-SEMI-001: only the *fitting* (query-in-reference) semi-global variant is provided; overlap / all-ends-free variants are not implemented. Scope choice, documented in XML + algorithm doc; no LIMITATIONS entry needed.

- GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001: the former limitations "no IUPAC / no reverse strand" are implemented (F19), not left as limitations. Left as documented scope: mismatch-tolerant motif search (fuzznuc `-pmismatch > 0`) = `ApproximateMatcher` / PWM units, not exact known-motif matching.

- GENOMIC-ORF-001: the former "not implemented" items (alternative start codons, non-standard genetic codes, nested vs. longest reporting, partial ORFs, NCBI length convention) are implemented (F21–F25), not left as limitations. **Done (F57, A1-18/A1-19):** ORFfinder's cross-frame "Ignore nested ORFs" (`-n`), `-b/-e`, the `-ml` clamp, `-strand`, the partial flags and tables 27/28/31 were measured on the real NCBI 0.4.3 binary (user-run Docker package) and implemented; `-c` (circular) is a no-op in the binary (identical output on 68 pairs), so nothing is implemented for it. Input scope: A/C/G/T `DnaSequence` or (A1-21, F50) an IUPAC nucleotide string through the string overload (ambiguous codons: stop only if every concrete codon is a stop, never a start).

- GENOMIC-REPEAT-001: none. The former "maximal-repeat classification not implemented" and "tie-break unspecified" are implemented (F29, F30). Left as documented scope: the reverse complement is not searched by `FindRepeats` (dispersed inverted repeats = `RepeatFinder.FindInvertedRepeats` / `FindReverseComplementRepeats`, B04).

- GENOMIC-SIMILARITY-001: none kept. Mash/sourmash strand-agnostic k-mers (`canonical`) are implemented (F33), not a limitation. Left as documented scope: MinHash sketching (Mash `-s`) is not needed for the exact index (`KmerAnalyzer` has the Mash distance); Jaccard distance = `1 - result/100`. Empty-union value is a convention (no source fixes it).

- GENOMIC-TANDEM-001: none kept. The former "exact only / brute-force / not implemented: approximate repeats" items are resolved: complexity fixed (F36), approximate repeats exist in-repo (F37). Left as documented scope: exact copies only (Benson's approximate copies = `RepeatFinder.FindApproximateTandemRepeats`); the unit is the left-end motif phase (MISA/pytrf), rotations are not canonicalised.

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (F126): the NEXUS reader implements the data block (dimensions, format datatype / interleave, matrix with quoted taxon names, comments) as written by Biopython / PAUP, not the whole Bio.Nexus grammar (trees, sets, charstatelabels, `matchchar`, parenthesised ambiguity); the Stockholm reader does not expose `alignment.operations`; both are documented in the XML docs, registered with A2-27c.
- ALIGN-GLOBAL-001 (F125): the Z-drop, approximate-mode and banded SIMD results of `Ksw2Aligner` are ported from the C source but unverified against the C program until the Docker package output is integrated (A2-29c); the exact cases are verified.

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (F127): `-go` / `-ge` on profiles of unequal size and gapped MSF / Clustal input crash or give garbage in the program itself (heap overflows, valgrind-confirmed); the port implements the intended algorithm for them and the oracle covers only the defined cases (equal-size merges, gap-free alignment files).

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 (F128): MSA / PO file input of `poa` and the standard-error progress lines are not ported (the latter print undefined `%ld` arguments); spoa / abPOA are not claimed.

## Cross-batch dedup requests

- ALIGN-STATS-001 (B05 finished-batch caller switch, tagged "B09 for B05"): `MotifFinder.AlignmentConsensus.cs` carried private copies of the EMBOSS `EDNAFULL` and `EBLOSUM62` tables (two `sbyte[,]` literals); both are now the canonical `SubstitutionMatrix.EdnaFull` / `Blosum62` (cell-for-cell equal, checked against the EMBOSS data files). **Done.** `MetagenomicsAnalyzer.Blosum62SelfScore` kept a 20-entry copy of the BLOSUM62 diagonal; now `SubstitutionMatrix.Blosum62.GetScore(r, r)` for the 20 standard residues (same values, non-standard residues still 0; (B09 for Metagenomics), **done**). `VariantAnnotator` "BLOSUM62-inspired scoring" is a heuristic, not a table copy, and `MhcflurryAffinityPredictor` uses BLOSUM62 as a network input encoding — different quantities, left for their batches.

- GENOMIC-COMMON-001: B05 request (`FindCommonRegions` per-start binary search → matching statistics) **done (F16)**. Note for B05 (no action needed): a public per-position matching-statistics method on `ISuffixTreeAnalysis` would replace the short-match `Contains` phase of `GenomicAnalyzer.MatchingStatistics`; the current implementation is exact and non-quadratic without it.

- GENOMIC-MOTIFS-001: B05 request (`FindMotif` unsorted) **done (F18)**; B06 request (`FindMotif` / `FindExactMotif` duplicate, KMER-POSITIONS-001) **done (F18)**: GenomicAnalyzer delegates to the canonical; no edit to the B05 file was needed. Note for B05/B06 (no action needed): `MotifFinder.FindExactMotif` still pays the suffix-tree build per `DnaSequence` for a single query (23 s / 1.7 GB at 10 Mbp); a size/"first query" policy there would need `DnaSequence` to expose whether its tree is built (B01 file) — results are already identical to `KmerAnalyzer.FindKmerPositions`.

- GENOMIC-ORF-001: B02 request (hard-coded stops → `GeneticCode`) **done (F21)**; B02/B11 request (three ORF engines → one core) **done for `GenomicAnalyzer` and `Translator.FindOrfs`** (new `Core/OrfScanner`, "(B09 for B02)": `Translator.FindOrfsInSequence` now calls it, one line of visibility change `HasDualCodingStopCodons` private → internal; no behaviour change, the B02 getorf-port oracle tests and all `Translator`/`Orf` tests green; one-line appended to B02.md Post-completion changes). **Open for B11:** `GenomeAnnotator.FindStartToStopOrfsInFrame` (L~140–190) is the same algorithm as `OrfScanner.FindRegions(nestedStarts: true, includeOpenEnded: false)` with the start set {ATG, GTG, TTG} (a predicate, not a table) and the stop test `GeneticCode.Standard.IsStopCodon`; it can become `OrfScanner.FindRegions(strand, code, c => StartCodons.Contains(c), nestedStarts: true)` + the aa-length filter `(End − 3 − Start)/3 ≥ minLength` and its own `OpenReadingFrame` mapping (forward coordinates `L − End`/`L − Start`, protein via `TranslateWithInitiatorMet`). Equality of the two outputs for ATG-initiated ORFs is locked by `Differential_GenomeAnnotatorFindOrfs_AtgInitiatedOrfsAreTheSameSet` (150 sequences); B11's input is a raw `string` that may contain N/IUPAC, which `OrfScanner` handles (ambiguous codon = stop only if all expansions stop; never a start) — the exact B11 behaviour on such input (`StartCodons.Contains(codon)` on the raw substring, `IsStopCodon`) is the same for A/C/G/T and ambiguous stops, so the switch is a caller change with no behaviour change; not done here because B11 is not a finished batch (its file is owned by that session). `GenomeAnnotator.FindStopToStopOrfsInFrame` (getorf `-find 0` leading/trailing regions) corresponds to `OrfStartCodons.AnySenseCodon, NestedOrfs = false, IncludeOpenEnded = true` only after the leading-region rule (frame starts open) — different model, left.

- (A2-46, WP23 2026-11-19; each item verified against the current code first, README rule 1; none of B18 / B21 / B19 is in QUEUE "Finished batches", so these are requests, not B09 edits)
  - **B18 (not finished) - open, a correctness defect:** `Analysis/ComparativeGenomics.cs:1198` `BestGappedFragmentMatch` calls `SequenceAligner.LocalAlign(fragment, reference, SequenceAligner.BlastDna)` - the LINEAR local aligner, which charges `GapExtend` (-2) per gap position and ignores `GapOpen` (-5) - while the summary says "gapped BLASTN best match (Goris 2007, pyani)": blastn charges `existence + k * extension` = 5 + 2k (`SequenceAligner.BlastDna` XML doc, BLAST+ manual). Planned call: `SequenceAligner.LocalAlignAffine(fragment, reference, SequenceAligner.BlastDna)` (identical to the BLAST scoring; linear-space automatically above 25 M cells). Consequence: the per-fragment identity / alignable fraction of `CalculateANI` change on gapped fragments (ANIb values shift), so it needs B18's own oracle (pyani / blastn on a gapped fragment) and its tests re-locked; also the 1020-nt fragment length of the ANIb definition is already `DefaultAniFragmentLength`. Not done here: behaviour change of a public method (README rule 3, "large").
  - **B21 (not finished) - note, no defect:** `Annotation/VariantCaller.cs:133` `CallVariantsCore` aligns with `GlobalAlign(reference, query)` (linear SimpleDna: +1 / -1 / -1 per gap position). B21's earlier request (an affine option) is satisfied by `GlobalAlignAffine(reference, query, scoring)` (tie order M > X > Y, same coordinates / rows contract as the linear one); B21 decides whether the default model should be affine (a variant-calling convention question).
  - **B19 (not finished) - note, no defect:** `Chromosome/ChromosomeAnalyzer.cs:909` / `:1192` (HOR monomer identity) use the linear `GlobalAlign` on monomers (~171 bp): a linear model is fine for identity, but every monomer pair is aligned with a full O(m n) DP and the result cached per pair; a banded / `ApproximateMatcher.EditDistance` identity would be cheaper. For the owner, not a B09 request.
  - **B07 (finished) - verified correct, nothing to do:** `MolTools/ProbeDesigner.cs:2511 / :2521` call `LocalAlignAffine(..., BlastDna)` - the affine model matching BLAST.
  - After B18 / B21 land, grep `LocalAlign(` / `GlobalAlign(` callers outside the batch again.

- (WP28, A2-14f, lead decision 2026-11-19; target batch B16 is NOT finished, so the request lives here) **ClustalW tree commands -> `PhylogeneticAnalyzer` (B16).** Verified: `ClustalWProgram.Run` throws `NotSupportedException` for `-TREE` / `-BOOTSTRAP` / `-OUTPUTTREE` / `-KIMURA` / `-TOSSGAPS` / `-PIM` / `-STATS`, and `Seqeron.Genomics.Phylogenetics/PhylogeneticAnalyzer.cs` has the generic pieces - `CalculatePairwiseDistance` (:291, p / JC / Kimura-2P / Hamming), `BuildNeighborJoining` (:495, private, `TreeBuildOptions`), `Bootstrap` (:1267, seed 42, resampling of alignment columns), `ToNewick` (:660) - but none of ClustalW's variants: the protein Kimura correction table (`dayhoff`-based `protDistance`), Tajima-Nei, gap tossing (`-TOSSGAPS`), ClustalW's `RandomGenerator` stream for `-SEED`, the node / branch bootstrap labels (`bootTotals`), the NEXUS / PHYLIP / DIST / PIM files. Planned call: B16 adds the ClustalW distance corrections and bootstrap resampling as additive options of `CalculatePairwiseDistance` / `Bootstrap` (+ a NEXUS / PHYLIP distance writer); B09's `ClustalWProgram` then maps the verbs onto them (the ClustalW NJ join order and rooting already exist in `SequenceAligner.GuideTree.cs`, F104). Evidence for the register: ClustalW `src/tree/{ClusterTree,UnRootedClusterTree,ClusterTreeOutput,RandomGenerator}.cpp` (Debian 2.1+lgpl-7); oracle = installed `clustalw` (>= 300 sets per option) once B16 implements them.
- (WP28, A2-14g, lead decision 2026-11-19; target batch B20 is NOT finished, so the request lives here) **ClustalW input formats -> `Seqeron.Genomics.IO` (B20).** Verified: `ClustalWProgram.Run` refuses PIR / NBRF, MSF (GCG), RSF, EMBL / Swiss-Prot, PHYLIP and NEXUS input with `NotSupportedException`; `Seqeron.Genomics.IO` has `FastaParser`, `EmblParser` (`EmblParser.cs:167` `Parse`: records with sequence + feature table, no alignment semantics) and in `SequenceIO.cs` `ParsePhylip` (:849, sequential / interleaved), `ParseClustal` (:963) - but no MSF / PIR / NBRF / RSF / NEXUS alignment readers (`grep -i "msf\|nexus\|pir"` in `Seqeron.Genomics.IO/**`: none). Planned call: B20 adds MSF, PIR, RSF and NEXUS alignment readers beside `ParsePhylip` / `ParseClustal` (name, residues, optional feature intervals; EMBL `FT` lines via `EmblParser` as feature-table masks) - B09's `ClustalWProfileFileReader` / `ClustalWProgram` then call them (and `ParsePhylip` for the PHYLIP-style input) instead of growing private parsers. Evidence: `fileInput/{PIRFileParser,MSFFileParser,RSFFileParser,EMBLFileParser}.cpp` of Debian clustalw 2.1+lgpl-7.

- ALIGN-MULTI-001 / GENOMIC-COMMON-001 (WP39, A4-6 / F155) -> B05: `SuffixTreeAlgorithms.FindMaximalExactMatches` (maximal-match enumeration shared by `FindCommonRegions`, `SeqMatchAll`, `MegaMerge`) collects every text occurrence of the longest match for every query position before the left-maximal ones are kept: O(n x m) time for repeats (poly-A 4 000 / 8 000 residues: 2.85 s / 10.8 s for 2n - 1 reported matches; 20 kb: 69 s), an unbounded list and no cancellation. Request (registered in QUEUE.md): a left-character summary per node to prune the enumeration to the left-maximal occurrences, an optional token and match limit. B09 side done: the list it receives is bounded (`WordMatchMaxMatches`), the rest of `MegaMerge` polls the token.

- `AnchorBasedAligner` is `internal`, only backs the explicitly approximate `MultipleAlignAnchored` (documented heuristic, comments truthful after F5); no published tool defines its output, so only the O(a^2) chain (A1-16) is actionable.

- Reverse-complement search in `FindRepeats` (B04 `FindInvertedRepeats`), approximate tandem repeats (B04 `FindApproximateTandemRepeats`), restriction catalog (B08), `cons` multi-sequence consensus statistics (B05 `GenerateEmbossConsensus`): canonical implementations exist elsewhere and are linked.
- pytrf/MISA comparison of the canonical scan (F38: `pip install pytrf` declined by the sandbox) is informational; the three-method Python oracle is complete (B04 F5 holds the MISA/pytrf comparison).

### Part 4 - defects and contract holes found by the correctness review of the round-1 code

- [x] (4e2f3ef7, WP23 2026-11-19: F80: one scoring contract (gaps <= 0, path score < 2^28, 64-bit sum of pairs), repro values rejected / exact, 7 tests.) **A2-37 DOABLE - scalar `ScoringMatrix` integer overflow gives a silently WRONG score; `int.MinValue` gaps crash:** `GlobalAlign("A"x50, "A"x50, new ScoringMatrix(1_000_000_000, -1, -2, -1)).Score` = 1 999 999 904 (true 5e10), `LocalAlign` = 2 000 000 000, `GlobalAlignAffine` = 1 999 999 900 - no exception; `GlobalAlignAffine(.., new ScoringMatrix(1, -1, int.MinValue, int.MinValue))` -> `IndexOutOfRangeException` (negation overflow; through `MultipleAlign(.., Affine)` it surfaces as `AggregateException`). The matrix path has the guard ("Score range too large for the integer dynamic program", SequenceAligner.cs:127) but the scalar `ScoringMatrix` (`implicit operator PairScoring`, SequenceAligner.cs:53) does not, and `ScoringMatrix` (AlignmentTypes.cs:23) validates nothing; also a POSITIVE gap score is rejected for `MatrixScoring` ("GapOpen must be a finite score <= 0") but accepted by the scalar path (`GlobalAlign("ACGT","AC", new ScoringMatrix(1,-1,5,5))` = 30). Fix: one range / sign validation shared by both paths (ArgumentException with the same message), `checked` arithmetic or a conservative bound `(|max score| + |gap|) * (m + n) < int.MaxValue`; tests with the exact repro values; also the MSA sum-of-pairs scorers (`ComputeSumOfPairsScore`, `SumOfPairsScore`) which accumulate in `int`.

### Part 5 - duplication hints for the sweep (NOT to be fixed now; the sweep must check the new code of this batch against each other and against the canonical code)

LEGIT = a separate implementation is required for exactness (the program's own arithmetic / tie order / single precision), list so the sweep does not "unify" it:
- Neighbour joining x 5: `PhylogeneticAnalyzer` NJ (canonical, B16), `SequenceAligner.GuideTree.cs:93/111` `ClustalWNeighborJoining`, `MuscleTrees.cs:410` `NeighborJoiningTree` (float, MUSCLE's own), `TCoffeeTree.cs:102` `TcNeighborJoining` and `:225` `TcNeighborJoiningFast`. LEGIT for the three programs (different tie rules, `float`, rooting, branch-length formulas; each oracle-exact against its binary); CHECK: `TcNeighborJoining` vs `TcNeighborJoiningFast` (two T-Coffee variants in one file) share the same pair scan - factor if the oracle stays identical; ClustalW's NJ returns `PhyloNode` (canonical type) - the other two have private tree classes (`MuscleTree`, `TCoffeeTreeNode`): candidates for a common rooted-tree interface + the canonical `PhylogeneticAnalyzer.ToNewick` / `ParseNewick` (L660 / L740) instead of `Muscle.cs:836 Newick`, `MuscleProgram.cs:727 ParseUserTree`, T-Coffee tree text.
- UPGMA / UPGMB: `MsaGuideTree.Upgma` uses the canonical builder (F53); `MuscleTrees.cs` UPGMB (MUSCLE's own, LEGIT) and the T-Coffee `upgma` mode (`TCoffeeTree.cs`): check both for reuse of the canonical UPGMA with `TreeBuildOptions`.
- Myers-Miller midpoint x 4: `LinearSpace.cs` (`LinearMidpoint` L196, `AffineMidpoint` L439; ours), `ClustalW.cs:467` (`FullPairwiseAlign::diff`, ClustalW's exact co-optimal choice, LEGIT), `ClustalWMultiple.cs:1557` `ProgDiff` (profile version, LEGIT), `TCoffeePrimary.cs:275/476` `MyersMillerState` / `TcMyersMiller` (T-Coffee's `util_dp_mm_nw.c`, LEGIT), `AnchorBasedAligner` (calls `GlobalAlignLinearSpace`, fine). The four are port-exact; the sweep should only confirm they do not duplicate the FILL kernels of the canonical linear/affine cores beyond the program-specific parts.
- Pair-fill / border helpers: `LinearAlignCore`, `AffineAlignCore`, the co-optimal fill (`GlobalCoOptimal.cs`, "shared fill/border" per F59), the Waterman-Eggert fill (`TopN.cs`), the general-gap fill (`GeneralGap.cs`), `AffineProfile` (`MultipleAffine.cs`) and the exact-MSA lattice: confirm one shared border / sentinel helper (`-inf` sentinel constants appear per file).
- Matrix readers / tables: `SubstitutionMatrix` (BLOSUM62 / PAM250 / EDNAFULL text tables), `SubstitutionMatrix.ClustalWSeries.cs` (352 lines of ClustalW series), `ClustalWUserMatrix.cs` (NCBI-layout reader), `MuscleMatrices.cs`, `TCoffeeMatrixTables.cs` (596 lines, generated) + `TcMatrix` in `TCoffeePrimary.cs`, the T-Coffee `blosum62mt` / `blosum50mt` vs `SubstitutionMatrix.Blosum62`; `MotifFinder.AlignmentConsensus` (done) - CHECK: are the T-Coffee / MUSCLE 23-letter triangles the same numbers as the canonical BLOSUM62 (LEGIT only where the program's table differs in the extra letters / `*` row / scaling); candidates for one `SubstitutionMatrix` loader + per-program views.
- k-mer / k-tuple distance x 4: `ClustalWQuick.cs` (k-tuple window / diagonal scoring, LEGIT), MUSCLE `MultipleAlignMuscle` k-mer distance (6-mer compressed alphabets, LEGIT), `TCoffeeKtupSimilarity` (`TCoffeeDistance.cs`), `SelectCenterSequence` 4-mer cosine (uses `CountKmersSpan`, done F6) vs `KmerAnalyzer`; sweep: confirm the three program k-tuple counters share one counting primitive where the arithmetic allows.
- Distances: `MultipleAlignmentStatistics.JukesCantorDistance` / `Kimura2ParameterDistance` (L354 / L362; EMBOSS `distmat` b = 3/4 / 19/20 - LEGIT constants) vs `PhylogeneticAnalyzer` `DistanceMethod.JukesCantor` / `Kimura2Parameter` (L107-110; DNA only): the DNA formula is the same; `Muscle.cs:1678 KimuraDist` (Dayhoff table above 75 % divergence, LEGIT); `PairwiseIdentityDistance` (SequenceAligner.cs) and `PercentIdentity` (:2115, T-Coffee `get_seq_sim`, LEGIT per DUP_MAP) vs `AlignmentStatistics.Identity` (B09 canonical) - three "percent identity" definitions: keep only the program-exact ones.

The hash of the query name seeds the tie order of chains of equal score (`hash ^= ...; Hash64Full(...)`, `GenRegs`), so for a first byte >= 0x80 the primary / secondary choice differs. Measured against `minimap2 2.26` (`Map` with `queryName` vs `minimap2 ref.fa q.fa` PAF, 60 random references with three equal copies of a 300-600 base segment, query = the segment): ASCII names 0 / 60 and 0 / 60 differ; names starting with `U+00E9` 57 / 60 differ, with `U+4E2D` 53 / 60 (e.g. port primary `0:802-1195`, program primary `0:202-595`); with the one-line fix `uint h = (uint)(sbyte)bytes[0];` (throwaway copy) 0 / 60 and 0 / 60. Also: C strings end at NUL, so a name containing U+0000 hashes only its prefix (port: whole string). The 1 320 committed PAF rows never use a non-ASCII or NUL-containing name (oracle gap). ### Part 3 - duplication hints for the sweep (refined; NOT to be fixed now)

LEGIT = the port's exactness (single precision, the program's own tie order / rounding / sort) requires a separate implementation; DEDUP-CANDIDATE = one implementation should serve.

- **DONE since round 4 (verified, no action):** the four `ajSeqIsNuc` copies are now ONE rule (`EmbossSequenceReader.IsNucleotide`: `grep ACGTUBDHKMNRSVWXY` finds only its doc comment); the EMBOSS plain-sequence cleaning is ONE helper (`EmbossSequenceReader.Clean` / `CleanGapped`).
- **DEDUP-CANDIDATE (new): `Minimap2Chaining.Hash64Full` (Minimap2Chaining.Hits.cs:154) is `Hash64(key, ulong.MaxValue)`** (Minimap2Chaining.cs:618): the same 64-bit integer hash of `sketch.c` / `hit.c` with an all-ones mask; keep one. `X31Hash` / `WangHash` (khash.h string / integer hashes) and the `Sketch` hash are LEGIT private helpers of the port (fix A5-1 in the one place).
- **DEDUP once A5-7 lands:** the `ajCvtSposToPosStart` / `-sbegin -send` position conversion (a local function `SposToPosStart` + `ZeroBased` in `MultipleAlignmentDisplay.ShowAlign`) and the per-program `EmbossText.Substring` clipping belong in `EmbossSequenceReader` with the qualifier code.
- **CHECK (new): three pair-HMM / Gotoh code paths inside the T-Coffee port:** `TcGotohProfile` (integer cost-callback Gotoh with the program's tie rules, TCoffeeDp.cs:643) vs the generic `SequenceAligner.GapProfile` / affine cores (LEGIT while the tie and border rules differ; CHECK that `gotoh_pair_wise` on a pure-identity cost equals `GlobalAlignAffine` on the 560 gap-model oracle sets); `TcViterbiMasks` (`viterbi_pair_wise`, TCoffeeViterbi.cs) vs the forward / backward pair-HMM of `TCoffeeProba.cs` and `ProbConsProgram` (shared `TcProbaModel` / emission tables? one transition / emission table set should serve all three; the Viterbi recursion itself is LEGIT).
- **CHECK `SequenceAligner.Merger.cs` (`embAlignPathCalc` + `embAlignWalkNWMatrix`) vs `SequenceAligner.Needle.cs` (`embAlignPathCalcWithEndGapPenalties`)** (unchanged from round 4: run both on the needle oracle with `endweight = false`; merge if equal on every pair).
- **CHECK `SequenceAligner.TopNSim.cs` (SIM) vs `TopN.cs` / `TopNLinearSpace.cs` (Waterman-Eggert)** (unchanged: the tie order is the documented difference; the DP kernels and the locate-interval helper may be shared). **CHECK `PairScoring.Window` / `InteriorOnly` (A4-8) vs the 16 `EndGaps` policies of `LinearSpace.cs`** (one policy table for the border vectors).
- **CHECK maximal-exact-match engines x 4** (`PairWordMatcher` Rabin-Karp words of seqmatchall / megamerger, `FindCommonRegions` suffix-tree MEMs, `AnchorBasedAligner.FindExactMatchAnchors`, `Minimap2Chaining.Sketch`): different outputs by design (LEGIT); CHECK that `PairWordMatcher` and the suffix-tree `-maxmatch` mode give the same match SET on the 330 seqmatchall pairs (a free cross-check; would let `FindNucmerClusters` and `SeqMatchAll` share one enumerator).
- **DEDUP-CANDIDATE pattern parsers x 2 (+ B14):** `FuzznucPattern.Parse` and `ProteinPattern.Parse` implement the same element grammar (classes, exclusions, repeats, ranges, anchors, `-` separators, the "bad repeat '(n,m)'" messages) twice in B09's own files (520 + 351 lines): one tokenizer with an alphabet policy; the PROSITE converter of B14 stays B14's (CHECK that fuzzpro `x(n,m)` and PROSITE `x(n,m)` agree on the shared subset).
- **DEDUP-CANDIDATE EMBOSS plumbing** (unchanged): the per-program input checks / `RequireEmbossRange`, `EmbossText.Substring`, the "sequence lengths" `RequireCells` guards and the `AlignmentCancellation` polls are repeated per port; with A5-2 / A5-7 the next ports add more: keep one helper set in `SequenceAligner.ResourceLimits.cs` / `EmbossText.cs` / `EmbossSequenceReader.cs`.
- **LEGIT** `NucmerClusters.UnionFind` (the only disjoint-set in the batch; mgaps' own union order decides the cluster ids), `Minimap2Chaining.Rmq.cs` (`LcElem` AVL tree: the tie behaviour of the range-minimum query is the tree's shape), `HeapAdjust` / `HeapMake` (ksort max-heap), `SdustCore` (the symmetric DUST intervals of `sdust.c`; `SequenceComplexity` of another batch scores windows, not intervals), `GlibcRandom` (ProbCons `rand`), the generated tables per program (MAFFT / Kalign / MUSCLE / T-Coffee / POA / ProbCons; CHECK the BLOSUM62 integer sub-table of `PoaMatrix.cs` / `MafftTables.cs` against `SubstitutionMatrix.Blosum62` by a test instead of a second literal).

### Part 4 - classification summary

DOABLE: A5-1 ... A5-10 (A5-1 is a proven defect with a one-line fix; A5-2 / A5-3 / A5-4 are guards, contracts and test quality of existing code; A5-5 / A5-6 are documentation and records; A5-7 ...
