### Part 2 - items (DOABLE unless stated)


**Defects, contract holes and resource problems of the code added in WP41 / WP42 (found by the fuzz / differential probes above):**

- [x] (WP43, 12f13370, F182: rolling 2-bit window + bottom-s set, output identical on 1 200 + 1 200 random inputs against the kept previous implementation, 20 Mbp 0.83 s / 0.5 MB (was 40 s / 4.1 GB), token on the builders, `MashCommands`, `mash_dist`, `mash_triangle`; B06.md Post-completion changes) **A6-1 - DOABLE (kind iii + ii) - `KmerAnalyzer.CreateMinHashSketch` / `CreateFracMinHashSketch` materialise EVERY distinct k-mer as a `string` (`HashSet<string>` / `CountKmers` dictionary) before hashing; `MashCommands.Dist` / `Triangle` and the MCP `mash_dist` / `mash_triangle` (A5-10) inherit it and have no cancellation poll.** Measured (Release build, random genome, `CreateMinHashSketch(g, 21, 1000)`): 1 Mbp 1.8 s / 285 MB peak, 5 Mbp 9.3 s / 1.2 GB, 20 Mbp 40 s / 4.1 GB (measured while other jobs shared the machine; the memory figure is the peak working set; an E. coli genome is 5 Mbp, a human chromosome extrapolates to tens of GB). `mash sketch` of the same 20 Mbp genome takes about a second in constant memory: Mash hashes the k-mers of a rolling window and keeps only the s smallest hashes (a bounded sorted set / heap, `Sketch.cpp` `MinHashHeap`), never the k-mer strings. The code is B06's file (additive, "B09 for B06"; the sketch builders are B09's F95 / F138 additions). Fix: build the canonical k-mer hash with a rolling 2-bit encoding (`KmerHashH1` of the canonical k-mer, the same `MurmurHash3` seed / 32-bit vs 64-bit rule), keep the bottom-s distinct hashes (and, for FracMinHash, the hashes <= `maxHash` with counts in a `Dictionary<ulong, long>` that holds only the retained hashes), poll `AlignmentCancellation` once per few thousand windows and per input file / pair in `MashCommands`, give `mash_dist` / `mash_triangle` a `CancellationToken` (every other long-running B09 tool has one, F86 / F157). Tests: the 440 + 660 existing oracle rows and the sourmash FracMinHash oracle must stay equal; new: 20 Mbp sketch allocates < 64 MB (allocation proxy, no wall clock), mid-run cancel at the n-th poll (`PollCancellation.CancelInside`).
- [x] (WP43, f49cacce, F177: `AcdCommandLine` + per-program qualifier universes, `EmbossSequenceQualifiers.ParseInputs`, 2 080 ACD-log rows + 640 report rows equal; the audit's unnumbered rule corrected: `-sbe 3` applies to every input) **A6-2 - DOABLE (kind i) - the `sequenceQualifiers` text of the 12 + 4 MCP tools / `EmbossSequenceQualifiers.Parse` accepts a strict subset of the programs' command-line grammar and IGNORES the rest SILENTLY.** Measured on EMBOSS 6.6.0: (a) the numbered form is accepted by a program with ONE sequence input: `fuzznuc a.fa -pattern ACG -sbegin1 5` = `-sbegin 5` (hits start at 5); the port parses `-sbegin1 5` with suffix `""` and drops it (`Parse("-sbegin1 5", "").IsDefault` is True), so `find_pattern_hits sequenceQualifiers="-sbegin1 5"` searches the whole sequence; (b) the unnumbered form `-sbegin 3 -send 6` after the two positional files of `needle a.fa b.fa` applies to the second sequence (qualifiers belong to the parameter they follow), the port ignores it for BOTH inputs (`Parse(.., "1")` and `(.., "2")` both default); (c) `-sbegin1=5` (the `=` form), the unambiguous abbreviations `-sbeg1 5`, `-sbe1 3`, `-sre2` and `-nosreverse2`-style negations after an abbreviation are accepted by the programs (`needle a.fa b.fa -sbeg1 3` = `-sbegin1 3`; `-sre2` reverses the second sequence) and rejected by the port with "Unknown sequence qualifier"; names are case-insensitive in both. Fix: one ACD-style qualifier matcher (case-insensitive, unique-prefix abbreviation among the qualifier names of the program, `name=value`, optional `-` / `--`, `-no<name>` for booleans), the number suffix `1` accepted for a one-input program, and an unnumbered qualifier of a two-input tool either applied as the program does after its second parameter (measured rule above) or refused with a message (never ignored); oracle rows built from COMMAND-LINE TEXTS (`generate_emboss_seqopts_oracle.py` gets a `text` column: numbered / unnumbered / abbreviated / `=` forms) over the 16 programs, MCP descriptions + docs regenerated (`sync_mcp_tool_docs.py`).
- [x] (WP43, 5b52e5f5, F178: any integer gap pair incl. -gapopen below -gapextend, ACD integer reset / refusal, `StandardError`, 872-row oracle; the audit's "refuse or follow" resolved: the program RESETS negative values with a warning) **A6-3 - DOABLE (kind i + ii) - `SequenceAligner.MatcherProgram` refuses every `-gapopen` below `-gapextend` and has no `-gapopen` / `-gapextend` validation.** EMBOSS matcher 6.6.0 accepts `-gapopen 0 .. 3` with the default `-gapextend 4` (`matcher a.fa b.fa -gapopen 0 -gapextend 4`: score 105, 4 gaps; `-gapopen 2`: score 97): the first gap residue then costs less than an extension (SIM's `q = gapopen - gapextend` is negative). The port computes `new MatrixScoring(matrix, -(open - gapExtend), -gapExtend)` and the scalar scoring rejects the positive `GapOpen` with "GapOpen must be a finite score <= 0 ... got 4". Differential sample (1 200 random pairs over `matcher` / `needle` / `water` / `stretcher` / `supermatcher` / `needleall`, gap pairs incl. open < extend, 0, 100 / 10): all rows of the other five programs equal; `matcher`: 67 / 139 equal, the 72 rows with open < extend (`(0,1) (0,4) (0,10) (1,4) (2,4) (2,10) (3,4)`) throw `ArgumentException`; the committed `matcher_sim_oracle.tsv` never uses open < extend (`EMBOSS_PROT_GAPS` pairs are all open >= extend: oracle gap). Contract: `gapOpen = 1e308` -> `OverflowException` ("Value was either too large or too small for a Decimal", from `TopNSim`), `gapExtend = -1e308` the same; `-gapopen 1.5` is "Invalid integer value" in the program (integers only; the port accepts any double) and `-gapopen -1` / `-gapextend -3` / `-alternatives 0` are "reset to 0 / 0 / 1" with a warning (the port throws `ArgumentException`, a documented stricter choice - keep or follow, but state it). Fix: accept a negative first-gap cost in the SIM core (`LocalTopNSimCore` / `TopNTieOrder.Sim` with a positive `GapOpen` score that is only legal there), integer + range validation (`RequireEmbossRange` 0..100 / 0..10 like supermatcher, finite, integral), rows with open < extend / 0 / 1 / 100 in `generate_matcher_sim_oracle.py` and the `emboss_seqopts_oracle.tsv` matcher group.

Tests: extend `SequenceAligner_NullContract_Tests` / `CrossBatchContract`-style one-assertion-per-entry-point tests with the parameter names.
- [x] (WP43, 5b52e5f5, F180: empty word table returns before the loops with `Error: no kmers found`; 260 rows; sibling programs checked) **A6-5 - DOABLE (kind ii + i) - `SequenceAligner.Supermatcher(.., wordLength = int.MaxValue)` spins for 30 s on two 23-base sequences (1.4 s for 100 000 000) and returns an empty result without the program's error.** `SupermatcherWords.Search` runs `wordLength - 1` modular multiplications and `wordLength` hash steps per query whatever the text length, with no cancellation poll. The installed `supermatcher` 6.6.0 does the same loop (`-wordlen 2147483647`: 8.9 s CPU, then "Error: no kmers found" and a segmentation fault; `-wordlen 100000000`: 0.5 s, "Error: no kmers found") but prints `Error: no kmers found` (stderr) for EVERY word length above the longest target (`-wordlen 24` / `30` with 23-base targets; `-wordlen 23` prints nothing); the port's `EmbossProgramResult.ErrorText` for `wordLength = 24 / 30` is only "No wordmatch start points for a vs b. No alignment", without the program's line (the oracle uses `-wordlen 3 .. 5` only). Fix: when `wordLength` exceeds the longest target return before the hash loops with the program's message (and an `AlignmentCancellation.Poll` per query / a bound for the loops of a word that cannot occur), a test for `wordLength = int.MaxValue` that finishes without the loop (iteration / allocation proxy, not wall clock) and oracle rows `-wordlen` above the target lengths (stderr compared). Check the same parameter of `SeqMatchAll` / `MegaMerge` / `DotMatcher` (the fuzz found no hang there).
- [x] (WP43, 12f13370, F182: `ArgumentException` with the program's WARNING / ERROR text (it says "input files", not the file name), 80 `error` oracle rows with statuses 1 and -8) **A6-6 - DOABLE (kind i) - `MashCommands.Dist` / `Triangle` print result lines for an input file whose records are ALL shorter than k; `mash dist` / `mash triangle` 2.3 refuse it.** `mash dist -k 11 -s 100 g.fa short.fa` (short.fa = one 3-base record): "WARNING: All fasta records in input files were shorter than the k-mer size (11)", empty stdout, exit status 1 (the same for the short file as reference, and for `triangle`); with `-i` the short record is simply not sketched (status 0, empty output: the port already agrees). The port compares the empty sketch and prints `a q 1 1 0/100`. The generator drops every run with a non-zero status (`generate_mash_commands_oracle.py: if r.returncode: continue`), so none of the 440 non-bounds rows has such a file (measured). Fix: `ArgumentException` with the program's message (file name included) when a non-`-i` input has no record of length >= k, new oracle group `error` (command, inputs, expected status + message) and the MCP tools' messages; extend the MCP tests.
- [x] (WP43, f78e1d38, F181: `MinimapOverrides` + `MinimapPresets.Apply(preset, overrides)`, nullable MCP parameters, 2 860 PAF texts equal; `-H` has no negation in 2.26: the audit's "-H off" corrected) **A6-7 - DOABLE (kind i + v) - MCP `minimap2_chains` with `preset`: an explicit parameter whose value equals its default CANNOT override the preset (documented in the parameter text: "every parameter left at its default value takes the preset's value"), and a boolean flag can only be switched ON by the caller.** In the program `-x PRESET` is applied first and every later option wins: `minimap2 -x asm5 -k 15` is k = 15 (preset k = 19), `--rmq=no` switches the asm presets' RMQ off, `--secondary=yes` re-enables the secondary hits of `sr`, `-H` off with `map-pb`, `-N 5` with `sr` (preset 20). `AlignmentTools.Minimap2.cs` merges with `Pick(given, default, fromPreset)` / `||` / `&&`, so `k = 15`, `rmq = false`, `outputSecondary = true`, `bestN = 5`, `homopolymerCompressed = false` are indistinguishable from "not given". The library API (`MinimapPresets.Apply(name) with { ... }`) is exact; only the MCP layer is lossy, and it repeats the field list of `MinimapPresets` a third time (the preset table, `ChainOptions`, the MCP merge). Fix: make the preset-sensitive parameters nullable (`int? k = null`, `bool? rmq = null`, ...), build `MinimapMapOptions` from `Apply(preset)` and apply only the given ones with `with` (the same order the program uses), regenerate the MCP documents (`sync_mcp_tool_docs.py`, the contract test checks defaults), tests with explicit-default overrides per preset (compare with `minimap2 -x asm5 -k 15` etc. through the preset oracle's `extra` option rows: add `-k` / `-w` / `--rmq=no` / `-N` / `-p` cases to `generate_minimap2_preset_oracle.py`).

### Part 3 - duplication hints for the sweep (code added since round 5 + refined round-5 list; NOT to be fixed now)

LEGIT = the port's exactness (single precision, the program's own tie order / rounding / sort / table) requires a separate implementation; DEDUP-CANDIDATE = one implementation should serve.

- **DEDUP-CANDIDATE (new): GFF3 attribute percent-encoding x 4.** `EmbossReportWriter.Escape` (Infrastructure; `% , ; = & \t \n \r`) vs `GffParser.cs:467-474` (IO; `Replace("%", "%25")` chain) vs `GenomeAnnotator.cs:724-742` (Annotation; spec set + control characters) vs `SequenceIO.ToGff` (`Uri.EscapeDataString`, a different encoding altogether): one `Gff3.EscapeValue` in IO / Infrastructure; EMBOSS's set is the spec set minus the other control characters (measure on the oracle: if the programs escape no more than the 8 characters the writer escapes, call the canonical function with a flag).
- **DEDUP-CANDIDATE (new): MCP `AnalysisTools.ToMashInputs` (AnalysisTools.Mash.cs:8-52) is a private FASTA reader** (header split at the first blank, record accumulation, whitespace removal) while `Seqeron.Genomics.IO.FastaParser` is referenced by `Seqeron.Mcp.Analysis.csproj`; the FASTA readers of the batch are now the five of the round-3 list plus this one: measure `FastaParser` on the 660 Mash oracle files and call it.
- **DEDUP-CANDIDATE (new): `EmbossInputSequence.ReverseComplement` vs `SequenceExtensions` / `DnaSequence.GetReverseComplementString`** (Core): the port keeps the case of every letter and maps `X -> N`, `U -> A`; CHECK that its 52-letter table equals the canonical complement for every other letter, then call the canonical function and patch `X` / case; the 1-based -> 0-based conversion `begin > 0 ? begin - 1 : begin` exists three times (`EmbossInputSequence.ReadCleaned` x 2, `MultipleAlignmentDisplay.ShowAlign.ZeroBased`): expose one `EmbossInputSequence.ZeroBased`.
- **CHECK (new): the set / pair readers.** `EmbossPairInput.Read`, `EmbossSetInput.ReadAll` / `SetIsNucleotide` / `DefaultMatrix`, `SeqMatchAll(..qualifiers)` (reads the set twice: once with the qualifiers, once with `Reverse = false, Begin = 0, End = 0`), `CalculateDistanceMatrix(..qualifiers)` (its own `nucleotide` decision from the first row) and the `MegaMerge` / `DotMatcher` overloads each re-implement "decide the type of the input from the forced type or the first member, read every member, pick the matrix": one entry point returning (views, protein, matrix).
- **CHECK (new): `MashCommands` vs `KmerAnalyzer`.** `MashCommands.Num` (C++ `cout << double`: `%g`, `inf` / `nan`) next to `CFormat.General`: the `nan` / `inf` rule belongs in `CFormat`; `MashCommands.Passes(c, v, d)` is a property of `MashComparison`; `Sketches` builds a sketch per input where `KmerAnalyzer.CreateMinHashSketch(IEnumerable<string>, ...)` already takes the records (and see A6-1: after the streaming rewrite the two sketch builders and the `-i` branch should share one core).
- **CHECK (new): the preset merge x 3** (`MinimapPresets.Apply`, `MinimapMapOptions.ChainOptions`, the MCP `Pick` block): resolved by A6-7; the four MCP report blocks (`ParseEmbossReportFormat`, the mutual-exclusion message, `plain` + recompute): resolved by A6-10.
- **LEGIT (new):** `EmbossReports.*` builders (program-specific feature tables) next to `EmbossAlignmentWriter` (alignment formats): different layers; `Minimap2Chaining.FormatPaf` is the only PAF writer of the repository (`grep -i paf` finds nothing else); `MashCommands.Bounds` uses the canonical `StatisticsHelper.BinomialCdf`.
- **Round-5 list, unchanged:** `Hash64Full` = `Hash64(key, ulong.MaxValue)` (Minimap2Chaining.Hits.cs:154 vs Minimap2Chaining.cs:618: still two functions), `TcGotohProfile` vs `GapProfile`, `TcViterbiMasks` vs the pair-HMM tables of `TCoffeeProba` / `ProbConsProgram`, `SequenceAligner.Merger.cs` vs `Needle.cs`, `TopNSim.cs` vs `TopN.cs` / `TopNLinearSpace.cs`, `PairScoring.Window` vs the `EndGaps` policies of `LinearSpace.cs`, the maximal-exact-match engines x 4, `FuzznucPattern.Parse` vs `ProteinPattern.Parse`, the EMBOSS plumbing (`RequireEmbossRange`, `EmbossText.Substring`, `RequireCells`, polls) repeated per port, the BLOSUM62 sub-table of `PoaMatrix.cs` / `MafftTables.cs` vs `SubstitutionMatrix.Blosum62` (test instead of a literal); outside the batch: `ComparativeGenomics.cs:1198`, R16 / R17 / R18.
